By centering perception at the edge, CLIFE provides a deployable foundation for downstream safety applications, while reducing bandwidth and calibration overhead for agencies operating multi-intersection corridors.

</details>


### [72] [Vision-Language Assistant for Emotional Reactions to Risky Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16181)
*Harine Choi,Eun Hak Lee,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Keep Yelling Assistant (KYA)系统，一个融合视觉感知与情感化语言生成的驾驶辅助框架。该系统使用YOLOv8变体实时检测危险驾驶行为（如突然切入），并通过大语言模型（ChatGPT-4o、Claude 3等）根据用户偏好生成不同情感基调（中性、幽默、分析型）的语音反应。在真实行车记录仪视频和108人用户研究中，YOLOv8s+ChatGPT-4o组合获得最高评分4.29/5.00，验证了情感智能车载AI的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶视觉语言模型虽然具备先进的感知与推理能力，但极少考虑情感维度或真实用户体验。本研究旨在弥补这一空白，将危险驾驶行为检测与情感化、个性化的语言反馈相结合，以提升驾驶员的意识、舒适度和信任感。

Method: 框架包含两个核心模块：(1)视觉模块：采用YOLOv8变体检测附近车辆并识别危险行为（如突然切入），提取并归一化相对距离、速度、预计到达时间等关键驾驶指标，生成结构化行为日志；(2)语言模块：将行为日志与用户定义的情感基调（中性、幽默、分析型）结合，利用ChatGPT-4o、Claude 3、Gemini 2.5、Copilot等大语言模型生成口头反应。通过行车记录仪视频和108人用户研究进行评估。

Result: 所有大语言模型均获得良好评分，但偏好因用户角色而异。YOLOv8s与ChatGPT-4o的组合取得了最高评分4.29/5.00。用户对不同情感基调的偏好呈现个性化差异。

Conclusion: KYA将真实世界感知与情感适应性对话相结合，为情感智能车载AI开创了新范式。该系统在提升传统车辆和自动驾驶车辆的安全性、信任感和情感福祉方面具有广阔前景。

Abstract: This study introduces a vision-language pipeline that detects risky driving behaviors and generates emotionally expressive responses to support driver awareness and comfort. Although vision-language models have advanced perception and reasoning in autonomous driving, existing systems rarely consider the emotional dimension or real-world user experience. Keep Yelling Assistant (KYA) detects high-risk driving maneuvers in real time, such as sudden cut-ins. It then produces emotional responses through a large language model tailored to driver preferences. The framework comprises two core modules. The vision module uses YOLOv8 variants to detect nearby vehicles and identify risky behaviors such as sudden cut-ins. Key driving metrics, including relative distance, speed, and projected reach time, are extracted and normalized to produce a structured behavior log. The language module processes this log with user-defined emotional tone settings, such as neutral, humorous, and analytical, and generates verbal reactions using state-of-the-art large language models, including ChatGPT-4o, Claude 3, Gemini 2.5, and Copilot. We evaluated the proposed system using dashcam videos containing risky driving behaviors and a user study involving 108 participants. Participants selected preferred response styles, and the large language models were evaluated based on emotional alignment. All models received favorable ratings, although preferences varied across personas. Notably, the combination of YOLOv8s and ChatGPT-4o achieved the highest score of 4.29 out of 5.00. By integrating real-world perception with emotionally adaptive dialogue, KYA introduces a new paradigm for emotionally intelligent in-vehicle artificial intelligence. It offers promising directions for improving safety, trust, and emotional well-being in both conventional and autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [73] [Searching Videos as Trees: Self-Correcting Agents for Grounded Long Video QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16189)
*Ce Zhang,Ziyang Wang,Yulu Pan,Oluwatumininu Oguntola,Pranav Wagh,Qiyu Wu,Hiromi Wakaki,Mohit Bansal,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoTreeSearch (VTS)，一种将Grounded长视频问答建模为迭代自纠正搜索的框架。VTS构建基于场景边界的非均匀时间树，训练智能体通过zoom_in、zoom_out、shift和answer四种离散操作进行导航，并引入包含故意绕路后恢复的轨迹合成pipeline进行监督微调与强化学习。在多个基准上显著超越此前方法，并验证了自纠正层级搜索的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的Grounded长视频问答方法使用单步crop_video操作进行粗到细定位，但缺乏从细到粗的回溯机制，导致智能体过早收敛、无法从早期错误中恢复。

Method: VTS包含三个关键组件：(1) 基于视觉场景边界构建非均匀时间树，每个节点对应语义连贯的视频片段；(2) 设计四种离散操作（zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, answer）实现显式回溯与恢复；(3) 轨迹合成pipeline生成包含故意绕路后恢复的多步路径，先进行监督微调，再结合定位与答案准确率奖励进行强化学习。

Result: 在CG-Bench上mIoU提升+12.5，Haystack-Ego4D上T-F1提升+7.4；在Video-MME、MLVU、LVBench等通用长视频QA上准确率提升最高+7.1。消融实验表明移除自适应下降或显式回溯会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 自纠正层级搜索是VTS性能提升的核心机制，将回溯建模为可学习的显式原语而非隐式行为，能有效解决早期错误累积问题，且学到的策略可迁移至通用长视频问答任务。

Abstract: Grounded long-video question answering (Grounded LVQA) requires answering a question about a long video while localizing the short evidence interval that supports the answer. Recent agentic methods frame this task as multi-turn exploration with a single crop_video(start, end) action, which supports coarse-to-fine narrowing but provides no primitive for fine-to-coarse backtracking. As a result, these agents typically converge prematurely and cannot recover from an early mistake. We propose VideoTreeSearch (VTS), a framework that casts grounded LVQA as iterative self-correcting search over an adaptive temporal tree. VTS constructs a non-uniform tree from visual scene boundaries so that each node corresponds to a semantically coherent segment, and trains an agent to navigate the tree through four discrete operations: zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, and answer. These operations expose backtracking and recovery as explicit, learnable primitives rather than implicit behaviors. To train this navigation, we introduce a trajectory synthesis pipeline that produces multi-step paths through the tree, including deliberate detours into incorrect branches followed by recovery. We use these trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, followed by reinforcement learning with grounding and answer-accuracy rewards. On three Grounded LVQA benchmarks (CG-Bench, Haystack-LVBench, Haystack-Ego4D), VTS outperforms the strongest prior agentic methods by +12.5 mIoU on CG-Bench and +7.4 T-F1 on Haystack-Ego4D. The learned policy also transfers to general long-video QA, surpassing all prior agentic baselines on Video-MME, MLVU, and LVBench by up to +7.1 accuracy points. Ablations confirm that self-correcting hierarchical search is the central mechanism behind these gains: removing either adaptive descent or explicit backtracking substantially degrades performance. Code is available at https://github.com/CeeZh/VTS.

</details>


### [74] [FVAttn: Adaptive Sparse Attention with Runtime Load Balancing for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16190)
*Hao Liu,Chenghuan Huang,Ye Huang,Zhiying Wen,Hao Liu,Mohan Zhang,Chen Li,Ziyang Ma,Jing Lyu,Jiangsu Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种免训练稀疏注意力系统，通过负载均衡、稀疏增强和计算重叠等运行时优化，解决了视频扩散Transformer中自适应稀疏注意力在序列并行下的负载不均问题，实现了4.41倍注意力加速和2.02-2.11倍DiT推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer处理长时空序列时，自注意力成为高分辨率视频生成的主要瓶颈。虽然免训练稀疏注意力可以降低开销，但自适应Top-p路由在多头GPU序列并行下会产生不均的逐头工作负载，导致稀疏注意力出现秩级别的拖后腿问题。

Method: 提出免训练稀疏注意力系统，前端采用Top-p路由、Top-k安全下限和视频感知块组织进行稀疏路由，并在运行时修复物化掩码。运行时负载均衡通过P2P通信迁移少量重载头以缩短关键路径；松弛感知稀疏增强利用非关键秩的松弛填充额外高价值块；计算重叠将调度和迁移开销隐藏在现有计算之后。

Result: 在步蒸馏Wan2.2 I2V上，平均负载不均衡从1.34降至1.08，相比FlashAttention实现4.41倍注意力加速，DiT推理加速达2.02-2.11倍，同时保持有竞争力的视频质量。

Conclusion: 通过运行时负载均衡与稀疏增强，有效解决了多GPU序列并行下自适应稀疏注意力的异构负载问题，实现了显著加速且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers process long spatio-temporal sequences, making self-attention the main bottleneck in high-resolution video generation. Training-free sparse attention reduces this cost, but adaptive Top-$p$ routing creates uneven per-head workloads under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. The resulting workload heterogeneity turns sparse attention into a rank-level straggler problem. We present \method{}, a training-free sparse-attention system that improves the distributed execution efficiency of adaptive sparse attention under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. \method{} uses Top-$p$ routing, a Top-$k$ safety floor, and video-aware block organization as the sparse-routing frontend, then repairs the materialized mask at runtime. Runtime Load Balancing migrates a small number of heavy heads via P2P communication to shorten the current critical path. Slack-Aware Sparse Augmentation fills residual non-critical-rank slack with additional high-value blocks, while overlap hides scheduling and migration overhead behind existing computation. On step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V, \method{} reduces average load imbalance from 1.34 to 1.08 and delivers a $4.41\times$ attention speedup over FlashAttention, while achieving a $2.02$--$2.11\times$ DiT inference speedup with competitive video quality.

</details>


### [75] [MotionForesight: Re-purposing Video Models for Future 3D Scene-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16192)
*Homanga Bharadhwaj,Yash Jangir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionForesight利用预训练视频模型中的先验知识，通过仅观察人类与物体交互的日常单目视频，无需任何物体属性假设，即可预测被操作物体上点的未来3D运动轨迹。该方法使用仅4万训练视频，在分布外物体、环境、视角和交互上实现泛化，性能超越使用超百万视频的更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可以通过被动观察推断物体可能的运动方式，这种对交互物理后果的预测能力是具身智能体在实际世界中行动所必需的。现有方法通常需要大量标注数据或对物体属性做出假设，本文旨在从日常单目视频中高效学习这种交互预测能力。

Method: 核心思想是将视频预测模型中的像素级先验重定向为未来3D场景流。具体包括：(1)基于预训练视频模型构建密集3D跟踪器，从完整视频片段生成伪真值轨迹；(2)仅使用观测帧训练预测器，将未来RGB和几何信息替换为学习到的掩码潜在表示；(3)冻结大型视频和跟踪组件，仅训练轻量级适配器将回顾性跟踪表征转化为前向预测器。

Result: 仅使用4万人类交互视频且无需语言等辅助输入，MotionForesight在分布外的各类物体、环境、视角和交互上均表现出良好的泛化能力，且性能优于使用超百万训练视频的更大规模模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可以高效地将视频先验重新利用为显式几何预测，为具身智能提供支持。这一方法展示了从被动观察中学习交互物理预测的可行路径。

Abstract: Humans can infer how objects are likely to move from passive observation: a cup may be lifted, a drawer may slide, and a lid may rotate shut. Such predictions expose the physical consequences of interaction needed to act in the real world. We study how to learn this anticipation from ordinary monocular videos of human-object interaction. Given a short observed video context, MotionForesight predicts future 3D trajectories for points on the manipulated object. This casts interaction prediction as object-centered 3D motion forecasting without any assumptions on the object properties. Our key insight is that video prediction models already encode rich priors about how objects move during human interactions. We redirect these priors from pixel prediction toward future 3D scene flow. We start from a dense 3D tracker built on a pretrained video model, generate pseudo-ground-truth tracks from complete clips, and train the forecaster using only the observed frames. We replace future RGB and geometry with learned mask latents and train a lightweight adapter to turn the retrospective tracking representation into a forward predictor, while freezing the large video and tracking components. Using just 40k human videos and no auxiliary inputs such as language, MotionForesight generalizes across diverse out-of-distribution objects, environments, viewpoints, and interactions. It also outperforms substantially larger models that use over a million training videos. These results show that we can efficiently re-purpose video priors into explicit geometric forecasts for embodied intelligence. https://motionforesight.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [Knowing the Self, Understanding the World: A Dual-Cognition Benchmark for UAV Spatio-temporal Reasoning with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16193)
*Like Liu,Zhengzheng Xu,Haitao He,Hongzhe Li,Shuchang Zhang,Dian Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了UAV-DualCog，一个面向无人机场景的双认知（自身状态与外部环境）多视角时空推理基准，包含图像和视频任务，实验表明现有MLLM在该基准上表现远不可靠，并验证了其作为评估基准和数据资源的双重价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机基准仅关注场景理解、事件识别或导航完成等单一视角，缺乏同时评估无人机智能体所需的双认知能力——即对无人机自身状态和外部环境在多视角时空上下文中的联合推理。

Method: 构建了一个从场景级语义点云自动生成数据的可扩展管道，生成包含多样场景、数百个地标和数千QA样本的基准；基准涵盖图像和视频两类任务，要求空间或时间定位而非简单的离散答案预测。

Result: 当前MLLM在无人机双认知任务上表现远不可靠，自身状态推理、视角变换、精确空间定位和时间间隔定位是持续瓶颈；额外验证了思考/前沿模型和人类基线，表明基准对人类可理解但对现有模型具有挑战性；从不相交场景构建的训练集通过轻量优化探针展示了结构化监督的有效性。

Conclusion: UAV-DualCog不仅是一个有挑战性的评估基准，还可作为推进基于MLLM的无人机智能体的数据资源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have achieved strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, yet their capabilities in UAV scenarios remain insufficiently explored. Recent UAV-oriented benchmarks have begun to evaluate MLLMs in aerial scenarios, but they typically focus on scene understanding, event recognition, or navigation completion, rather than jointly assessing the dual-cognition capability required for UAV agents: reasoning about both the UAV's own state and the external environment in multiview spatio-temporal contexts. To address this gap, we present UAV-DualCog, a benchmark for aerial multiview spatio-temporal reasoning built on this dual-cognition perspective. UAV-DualCog includes both image and video tasks to jointly evaluate self-state and environment-state reasoning, while requiring spatial or temporal grounding beyond discrete answer prediction. We also develop an automated pipeline that constructs data from scene-level semantic point clouds, yielding a scalable benchmark with diverse scenes, hundreds of landmarks, and thousands of QA samples. Extensive evaluations show that current MLLMs remain far from reliable in UAV dual cognition. Self-state reasoning, viewpoint transformation, precise spatial grounding, and temporal interval localization are persistent bottlenecks, and additional validation with thinking/frontier models and a human baseline confirms that the benchmark is understandable to humans but challenging for existing models. We further construct UAV-DualCog-Train from disjoint scenes and show through a lightweight optimization probe that it provides useful structured supervision, suggesting its value not only as an evaluation benchmark but also as a data resource for advancing MLLM-based UAV agents. Project website and supplementary materials: https://uav-dualcog.lozumi.com

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Large Language Model-Enhanced Multi-hop Parallel Image Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15297)
*Bingyan Xie,Jihong Park,Rui Mao,Longyu Zhou,Tianhao Liang,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种大语言模型增强的多跳并行图像语义通信框架（LLM-MHPSC），通过残差补偿链路、粗到细残差压缩方案、LLM残差传输优化器和自适应跳数选择策略，在多跳无线图像传输中有效缓解失真累积，以边际带宽增加实现鲁棒传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单跳语义通信方案在多跳无线图像传输中存在失真累积问题，需要一种能够有效缓解逐跳失真累积的新型通信框架。

Method: 1) 每跳部署额外残差补偿链路以抵消累积失真；2) 结合深度学习压缩器和自适应算术编码的粗到细残差压缩方案；3) 基于大语言模型的残差传输优化器（LLM-RTO），估计残差分布并实现信道状态和跳数感知的码率调整；4) 自适应跳数选择策略，按需激活残差链路。

Result: 优于现有最先进的语义通信和传统方案，以边际带宽增加实现鲁棒图像传输。

Conclusion: 该方法为将语义通信扩展到实际多跳应用场景提供了一种灵活有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a large language model-enhanced multi-hop parallel image semantic communication (LLM-MHPSC) framework to mitigate distortion accumulation in multi-hop wireless image transmission. Unlike conventional single-hop semantic communication schemes, LLM-MHPSC deploys an extra residual compensation link at each hop to counteract accumulated distortions. To minimize additional bandwidth overhead, a coarse-to-fine residual compression scheme is designed by integrating a deep learning-based compressor with adaptive arithmetic coding (AAC). Furthermore, a large language model-based residual transmission optimizer (LLM-RTO) is developed to accurately estimate residual distributions and enable channel state and hop-aware rate adjustment, thereby improving residual compression efficiency under varying channel and hop conditions. An adaptive hop selection strategy is also proposed to activate the residual link on demand, striking a balance between transmission performance and computational cost. Experimental results show that LLM-MHPSC outperforms state-of-the-art semantic communication and traditional schemes, realizing robust image transmission with a marginal increase in bandwidth. This framework provides a flexible and effective solution for extending semantic communication to practical multi-hop application scenarios.

</details>


### [78] [Data-driven Video Codec with Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15298)
*Nishan Khanal,Saugat Neupane,Abhinav Chalise,Nimesh Gopal Pradhan,Dinesh Baniya Kshatri*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出将视频和音频联合编码为单个正弦表示网络（SIREN）的权重，通过知识蒸馏、权重量化和无损压缩实现压缩，在测试视频上达到2.61倍压缩比，并对比传统编解码标准分析了不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种非传统的视频音频联合压缩方法，利用隐式神经表示（INR）取代基于像素和采样的传统编解码范式。

Method: 使用SIREN网络将时空坐标映射到RGB值和音频振幅；设计分离的初始化层、共享隐藏层及三个输出分支（视频+双路Siamese音频分支用于噪声估计与消除）；过拟合教师网络后通过基于响应的知识蒸馏压缩为学生网络，再经16-bit对称权重量化和LZMA2无损编码。

Result: 6.08 MiB测试视频上：视频PSNR 28.72 dB、SSIM 0.75；音频PSNR 24.18 dB、log谱距离10.69 dB；表示从9.05 MiB压缩至2.33 MiB，压缩比2.61。1-32 bit量化扫描显示16 bit达到饱和。

Conclusion: 该方法在压缩比和重构质量上仍不及H.264、HEVC和MP3等传统标准，但展示了基于INR的联合编解码可行性与浏览器端原型实现。

Abstract: A conventional codec stores a video as compressed pixel data. We instead store the video, together with its audio track, as the weights of a single sinusoidal representation network (SIREN) that maps space-time coordinates to RGB values and audio amplitudes. The network uses separate audio and video initialization layers, a stack of shared fully connected hidden layers, and three output branches: one for video and two Siamese audio branches whose disagreement is used to estimate and subtract residual noise. The overfitted teacher network is then compressed by response-based knowledge distillation into a smaller student, followed by 16-bit symmetric weight quantization and lossless LZMA2 (xz) encoding. On a 6.08 MiB test video, the quantized student reaches a video PSNR of 28.72 dB with SSIM of 0.75, and an audio PSNR of 24.18 dB with a log spectral distance of 10.69 dB, while the pipeline shrinks the representation from 9.05 MiB to 2.33 MiB, an overall compression ratio of 2.61. A bit-width sweep from 1-bit to 32-bit quantization shows that reconstruction quality saturates at 16 bits. We compare against H.264, HEVC, and MP3, report where the approach falls short of them, and describe a browser-based prototype that trains, transfers, and decodes these models over WebRTC.

</details>


### [79] [MSTF-Net: A UAV-Oriented Multi-Spectral Video Segmentation Method via Modality-Robust, Scale-Adaptive, and Consistent Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15628)
*Chenwei Wang,Zhida Wang,Zelin Li,Elif Ozden-Yenigun,Houde Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSTF-Net，一种面向多光谱视频分割的模态鲁棒尺度自适应融合框架，通过MSCSE模块（跨模态注意力+残差引导的模态噪声抑制）和MTCSC模块（多尺度时域语义一致性）解决RGB-热成像融合中的模态差异和时序变化问题，在MVSeg和CART数据集上分别达到56.42%和51.80% mIoU，实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光谱视频分割在无人机应用中面临两大挑战：(1) RGB与热成像模态间的显著特征差异导致模态融合困境，互补信息难以有效利用；(2) 无人机平台快速运动和视角变化引发时序变化，造成帧间外观不一致和错位问题。

Method: 提出MSTF-Net框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) MSCSE模块通过跨模态注意力生成统一的实例查询，并利用残差引导的差异滤波与一致性约束抑制模态特定噪声；(2) MTCSC模块根据帧距离自适应调整时域感受野，同时捕获粗粒度全局上下文和细粒度局部结构信息。

Result: 在MVSeg数据集上达到56.42% mIoU，在CART数据集上达到51.80% mIoU，特别是在小目标、遮挡和模态退化等挑战性场景下表现优异，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: MSTF-Net通过模态鲁棒的尺度自适应融合框架有效建模了跨模态融合与时域一致性，显著提升了多光谱视频分割在复杂条件下的性能。

Abstract: Multi-spectral video segmentation is essential for robust scene understanding in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) applications such as city planning, land use monitoring, traffic monitoring, and crowd estimation. While the fusion of RGB and thermal modalities offers complementary information for perception under varying lighting and visibility conditions, two fundamental challenges remain: (1) the modal fusion dilemma, arising from significant discrepancies between RGB and thermal features that obscure complementary cues, and (2) temporal variation, induced by rapid motion and viewpoint changes on UAV platforms, which leads to appearance inconsistency and misalignment across frames. To address these issues, this study proposed MSTF-Net, a modality-robust scale-adaptive fusion framework for multi-spectral video segmentation that effectively models cross-modal fusion and temporal consistency. The Modality Spatial Complementary Suppression and Enhancement (MSCSE) module generates unified instance queries via cross-modal attention and suppresses modality-specific noise using residual-guided discrepancy filtering and consistency constraints. To model temporal dynamics, the Multi-scale Temporal Cross-modality Semantic Consistency (MTCSC) module adaptively adjusts the temporal receptive field based on frame distance, capturing both coarse global context and fine local structure across time. Extensive ablation experiments on public RGB-T datasets demonstrate that MSTF-Net achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance, especially under challenging conditions such as small targets, occlusion, and modality degradation. Specifically, reached 56.42\% mIoU on the MVSeg dataset and 51.80\% mIoU on the CART dataset, respectively.

</details>


### [80] [Robust Monitoring of Arc Welding Processes: A Generalizable Framework with DVAE and Particle Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16013)
*Yue Cao,Hai Lin,YuMing Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合无监督深度潜表示学习（动态变分自编码器DVAE）与贝叶斯滤波（粒子滤波PF）的通用弧焊监控框架，无需特定工艺调参即可在GTAW和GMAW上实现鲁棒的状态监测，解决了传统深度学习方法依赖大规模标注数据和可扩展性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弧焊过程易受干扰导致焊缝质量下降，实时监控因视觉模式复杂和动态非线性而困难；深度学习虽有效但依赖大量标注数据和特定调参，缺乏通用性和可扩展性。本文探索能否用统一方法跨工艺进行状态监测。

Method: 提出DVAE-PF框架：DVAE由CNN编码器-解码器和LSTM转移模型组成，联合学习潜表示及其在控制输入下的演化；为抑制传感器噪声和干扰，采用专用粒子滤波传播潜变量和LSTM隐藏状态，实现鲁棒实时推理。

Result: 在GTAW和GMAW两种工艺上验证，无需工艺特定调参，结果表明框架具有良好通用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 无监督深度潜表示学习与贝叶斯滤波结合可有效应对弧焊的复杂干扰，为跨工艺通用实时监控提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Arc welding processes are essential for continuous fabrication but prone to disturbances that impair weld quality, making real-time monitoring critical yet difficult due to complex visual patterns and nonlinear, time-varying dynamics. Deep learning shows promise but faces scalability limits because of its dependence on large labeled datasets and application-specific tuning. We explore whether a unified approach can characterize major arc welding processes across applications and improve scalability through consistent state monitoring. This paper introduces a robust and generalizable monitoring framework for arc welding. It combines unsupervised deep latent representation learning, which extracts compact features from weld pool images, with Bayesian filtering to handle persistent and fluctuating disturbances such as arc radiation and specular reflections. Specifically, a Dynamic Variational Autoencoder (DVAE), consisting of a CNN-based encoder-decoder and an LSTM-based transition model, jointly learns latent representations and their evolution under control inputs. For robust real-time inference, a specialized Particle Filter (PF) propagates the latent and LSTM hidden states, preserving process history while suppressing sensor noise. This design is well suited to welding's slow and inertial dynamics. Validation on GTAW and GMAW without process-specific tuning demonstrates the framework's generalizability and robustness.

</details>

### [81] [Xiaomi-Robotics-1: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models with over 100K Hours of Real-World Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15330)
*Xiaomi Robotics Team,Jun Guo,Piaopiao Jin,Jason Li,Peiyan Li,Yingyan Li,Futeng Liu,Wanli Peng,Optimus Qin,Yifei Su,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Runze Suo,Heyun Wang,Yunhong Wang,Rujie Wu,Caoyu Xia,Lina Zhang,Jack Zhao,Guoliang Chen,Wenlong Chen,Xinze He,Bin Li,Qing Li,Zhuorong Li,Heng Qu,Wenxuan Song,Diyun Xiang,Yifan Xie,Peiran Xu,Hangjun Ye,Wen Ye,Han Zhao,Quanyun Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 小米提出Xiaomi-Robotics-1，一个基础视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过两阶段训练（预训练+后训练），在超过10万小时真实操作轨迹上预训练，并在后训练中与机器人本体和人类指令对齐。该模型表现出强扩展性，在RoboCasa365上达到57.6%成功率（SOTA），在RoboDojo上平均得分20.07（远超此前SOTA 13.07）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在泛化到未见环境和适应新下游任务时存在局限性，缺乏既能开箱即用又能高效微调的基础机器人策略。

Method: 提出两阶段训练方案：(1) 预训练阶段，利用UMI设备采集的超过10万小时真实操作轨迹，通过可扩展的自动标注流水线用自然语言描述场景状态转换，训练模型具备广泛可泛化的动作生成能力；(2) 后训练阶段，将预训练能力与机器人本体和人类自然使用的指令式语言对齐。

Result: 模型展现出强扩展性，随数据规模和模型尺寸一致提升。在RoboCasa365上以57.6%成功率超越此前SOTA（46.6%），在RoboDojo上平均得分20.07，显著优于此前SOTA（13.07）。

Conclusion: Xiaomi-Robotics-1是一个强大的机器人基础策略，能够开箱即用地在未见环境中执行多样化移动操作任务，同时可通过极少微调数据高效适应复杂下游任务，并建立了新的SOTA性能。

Abstract: We present Xiaomi-Robotics-1, a foundational vision-language-action (VLA) model capable of (1) following diverse language instructions to perform a wide range of mobile manipulation tasks in unseen environments out-of-the-box, and (2) efficiently adapting to novel downstream tasks with minimal fine-tuning data. We propose a two-stage training recipe consisting of pre-training and post-training. During pre-training, we imbue the model with broad and generalizable action-generation capabilities by training on over 100k hours of real-world manipulation trajectories collected via UMI devices. Crucially, we develop a scalable auto-labeling pipeline that annotates trajectory clips with natural languages describing scene state transitions, providing rich and precise conditioning for action learning. During post-training, we aim to align these capabilities with robot embodiments and imperative instructions that humans naturally use to prompt robots. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong scaling behavior. Xiaomi-Robotics-1 consistently improves with increased data scales and model sizes during pre-training. This scaling behavior directly transfers to post-training, where a stronger pre-training model yields better out-of-the-box real-robot performance in unseen environments. Furthermore, Xiaomi-Robotics-1 serves as a strong robot foundation policy that can be efficiently fine-tuned on complex, dexterous tasks with high data efficiency. Across multiple simulation benchmarks, Xiaomi-Robotics-1 outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Notably, it establishes a new state-of-the-art with a 57.6% success rate on RoboCasa365, surpassing the previous best of 46.6%. Furthermore, it achieves an average score of 20.07 on RoboDojo, significantly outperforming the prior state-of-the-art (13.07). Code and model checkpoints will be released. Project page: https://robotics.xiaomi.com/xiaomi-robotics-1.html

</details>


### [82] [NeuroCommitSSM: Decision-Centric Shared Autonomy for Safe Assistive Manipulation via EEG-EMG-ET Commit Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15395)
*Tipu Sultan,Param Sangani,Kody Cool,Pascal Sikorski,Guangping Liu,Hadi Akbarpour,Madi Babaiasl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuroCommitSSM是一个面向辅助机器人操作的安全决策框架，通过同步EEG、EMG和眼动追踪信号预测连续的就绪分数，并采用三状态有限状态机（HOLD-ASSIST-COMMIT）进行门控执行。在32名受试者的5项日常生活任务评估中，达到0.950的动作平衡准确率，且每1000个REST窗口仅产生0.75次误提交事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助机器人操作研究主要关注"做什么"（如抓取目标），而忽略了"何时执行"这一关键决策问题。不恰当的执行时机可能导致误操作、用户不信任甚至安全隐患。因此，需要一个能够融合神经生理信号与机器人状态可行性检查的决策框架，实现安全、及时的提交执行控制。

Method: 1) 多模态信号融合：同步采集EEG、EMG和眼动追踪信号，预测连续的就绪分数c_t∈[0,1]；2) 离散事件转换：通过驻留时间(dwell)和迟滞滤波(hysteresis)将连续分数转为离散提交事件；3) 三状态有限状态机HAC：HOLD（保持）-ASSIST（辅助）-COMMIT（提交），同时要求神经模型输出持续的就绪信号以及实时感知与机器人状态可行性（目标可见性、逆运动学可解性、无碰撞规划）后才启动运动。

Result: 在N=32名受试者、5项ADL任务上，采用LOSO交叉验证和7种传感器丢失场景(S0-S6)评估，NeuroCommitSSM达到0.950动作平衡准确率、0.75 FP/1k REST；在仅EEG条件下，平衡准确率0.785、FP/1k REST仅0.29，而TCN基线在同一条件下产生99.95 FP/1k REST。HIL验证表明可行性检查减少了误启动和决策不稳定性且不牺牲任务成功率。

Conclusion: NeuroCommitSSM通过神经生理信号与机器人状态可行性检查相结合的方式，有效解决了辅助机器人操作中"何时执行"的安全决策问题。该方法在保持高准确率的同时大幅降低误提交事件，即使在传感器缺失等退化条件下仍能保持鲁棒性，优于TCN基线方法，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: We present NeuroCommitSSM, a decision-centric framework that models when to execute, not just what to do, for safe commit-to-execute control in assistive robotic manipulation. NeuroCommitSSM predicts a continuous commit-readiness score c_t in [0,1] from synchronized electroencephalography (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and eye-tracking (ET), and converts it into discrete commit events through dwell and hysteresis filtering. A three-state finite-state supervisor, HOLD-ASSIST-COMMIT (HAC), gates execution by requiring both a sustained commit-readiness signal from the neural model and real-time perception and robot-state feasibility, including target visibility, inverse kinematics solvability, and collision-free planning, before initiating motion. We evaluate the framework on N=32 subjects performing five activities of daily living (ADL) tasks aligned with the International Classification of Functioning, Disability and Health (ICF), using leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation and seven sensor-dropout scenarios (S0-S6). NeuroCommitSSM achieves 0.950 action-balanced accuracy with 0.75 false commit events per 1000 REST windows (FP/1k REST), and maintains low false commits and stable state transitions under sensor loss. For example, in the EEG-only condition, it achieves 0.785 balanced accuracy and 0.29 FP/1k REST, whereas the Temporal Convolutional Network baseline produces 99.95 FP/1k REST under the same condition. Hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) validation on a Kinova Gen3 arm shows that feasibility-checked execution reduces false starts and decision instability without sacrificing task success. Supplementary materials, including code, datasets, videos, and additional analyses, are available at https://madibabaiasl.github.io/NeuroCommitSSM/.

</details>


### [83] [Robust Silicone Pour Casting and Sensor Embedding Procedures for Soft Robotic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15422)
*Harshit Thakker,Paul Dela Cruz,Mostafa Mo. Massoud,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于两部分浇注铸造的软体气动执行器稳健、可重复且可扩展的制造流程，解决了内腔堵塞和气密密封问题，并嵌入薄膜柔性传感器实现精确弯曲角度测量。通过有限元建模、PID压力控制实验和自动图像处理校准，验证了执行器的重复性和低滞后性能。两名操作员成功制造24次，证明了方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人在康复和手术等领域具有优势，但其可重复和可扩展的制造难题限制了实际部署。现有制造方法多依赖人工且易出错，因此需要一种鲁棒、可重复、可扩展的制造方案，以推动软体机器人的广泛应用。

Method: 采用两部分浇注铸造法制造软体气动执行器，设计防止内腔堵塞和气密密封的工艺；嵌入薄膜柔性传感器并利用自动图像处理进行弯曲角度标定；结合有限元建模(FEM)分析内压下的应力和变形；使用PID压力控制进行气动驱动实验，通过阶梯和正弦波形测试验证性能。

Result: 两名操作员成功完成24次 fabrication，执行器在阶梯输入下表现出可重复的角度响应，正弦输入下仅有少量滞后（与粘弹性响应一致），仿真与真实弯曲角度结果吻合。

Conclusion: 提出的制造方法具有可重复性和鲁棒性，结合仿真与实验基准测试，有助于推动软体机器人技术的更广泛采用。

Abstract: Soft robots are well-suited for applications such as rehabilitation and surgery that require adaptable and safe interaction with their environment. However, the challenges of reproducible and scalable fabrication of soft robots limit their real-world deployment. Various fabrication methods have been introduced, but many are labor-intensive and prone to human error. Therefore, traditional two-part pour casting remains an attractive option. This paper presents procedures for robust, repeatable, and scalable fabrication of soft pneumatic actuators using two-part pour casting. The presented methods prevent internal cavity clogging and ensure air-tight sealing. Additionally, a robust sensor embedding procedure for thin-film flex sensors is presented, which allows for accurate and repeatable data acquisition. Finite Element Modeling (FEM) of the soft actuator is performed to analyze stress and deformation from internal pressure loadings. Pneumatic actuation experiments with PID pressure control are performed. Automated image processing is used to calibrate the embedded flex sensor to bending angle measurements. Staircase and sinusoidal profile actuation experiments validate the performance of the fabricated actuator. Angle response experiments for the staircase input show repeatable performance, and the sinusoidal input shows a small amount of hysteresis consistent with viscoelastic response to pneumatic actuation of soft actuators. Simulated and real-world bending angles show comparable response. These methods provide a repeatable and robust fabrication procedure, validated across two operators and 24 successful fabrications, along with benchmark simulations and experimental testing. These benchmarks will enable more widespread adoption of soft robotics.

</details>


### [84] [VTAP Gripper: Synergizing Fingertip Sensing and a Visuo-Tactile Active Palm for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15448)
*Yuhao Zhou,Sheeraz Athar,Zhixian Hu,Binghao Huang,Yunzhu Li,Juan Wachs,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种触觉反应式夹爪，通过集成视觉-触觉主动手掌（VTAP）和配备触觉阵列传感器的可重构柔性手指，利用结构化手指-手掌协同与多模态感知，实现了鲁棒抓取和精细操作。进一步提出了分阶段、手势条件化的重定向框架用于灵巧遥操作。实验验证了系统在多种挑战性任务中的高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作方案多依赖高自由度拟人化设计，结构复杂且难以在抓取鲁棒性与精细操作能力之间取得平衡。本文旨在通过结构化手指-手掌协同和多模态感知，以较低的机械复杂度实现高性能的抓取与操作。

Method: 设计了VTAP（视觉-触觉主动手掌），集成了双模态手掌（结合视觉定位与触觉反馈）、可重构柔性手指（带触觉阵列传感器），以及分阶段、手势条件化的重定向框架，用于将人手运动映射到异构三指结构上。

Result: 在YCB物体和易碎物体反应式抓取、注射器针筒在手中的重新定向与推压操作、小至3mm聚类物体的分离、以及视觉-触觉结合的轴孔插入等任务中均取得了成功验证。

Conclusion: 通过手指-手掌协同和多模态感知，无需高自由度拟人化设计即可实现高操作性能。VTAP夹爪及其重定向框架为灵巧夹爪设计、操作及基于学习的接触丰富数据采集提供了实用的参考架构。

Abstract: This paper presents a tactile-reactive gripper that integrates a Visuo-Tactile Active Palm (VTAP) and compliant, reconfigurable fingers equipped with tactile array sensors. The design exploits structured finger-palm synergy and multi-modal perception to achieve both robust grasping and fine manipulation. The actuated bi-modal palm seamlessly combines long-range visual localization with contact-rich tactile feedback, substantially extending the system's manipulation capability. To bridge the embodiment gap between human hand motion and the heterogeneous three-finger structure, we further propose a staged, gesture-conditioned retargeting framework for dexterous teleoperation. Extensive experiments validate the system across a range of challenging tasks: reactive grasping of YCB and fragile objects, in-hand syringe reorientation and plunger actuation, singulation of clustered objects down to 3 mm in diameter, and vision-tactile peg-in-hole insertion. Results demonstrate that high manipulation performance can be achieved through coordinated finger-palm interaction and multi-modal sensing, without resorting to high degrees of freedom anthropomorphic designs. The VTAP gripper and its retargeting framework offer a practical reference architecture for dexterous gripper design, manipulation, and contact-rich data collection in support of learning-based approaches. Project webpage: https://yuhochau.github.io/vtap/.

</details>


### [85] [Risk-Aware Preference Learning for Stochastic Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15483)
*Yi-Shiuan Tung,Yuni Wu,Wei Jiang,Alessandro Roncone,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在社交机器人导航中，使用累积前景理论(CPT)代替期望效用理论(EU)来建模人类风险敏感偏好，可以显著降低从偏好中学习奖励函数的遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人类偏好学习奖励函数的方法大多假设人类使用期望效用(EU)线性聚合概率与效用，但行为证据表明人类存在系统性风险敏感偏差（如高估小概率负事件、损失厌恶）。这种不匹配在安全关键的社交机器人导航场景中可能导致严重后果，因此需要研究如何更好地建模人类真实偏好。

Method: 在Bradley-Terry偏好学习框架下，比较期望效用(EU)与累积前景理论(CPT)两种偏好模型。CPT是一种能够捕捉人类风险敏感性的非线性决策模型。通过风险敏感用户生成的偏好数据进行初步实验，评估两种模型恢复的奖励函数性能。

Result: 当偏好数据来自风险敏感用户时，基于CPT的学习者恢复的奖励函数相比基于EU的学习者具有显著更低的遗憾值。

Conclusion: 在从随机机器人结果偏好中学习奖励时，建模人类风险敏感性至关重要。

Abstract: Learning reward functions from human preferences is a widely used approach for aligning robot behavior with user expectations in human-robot interaction. Most existing approaches assume that humans evaluate uncertain outcomes using expected utility (EU), aggregating outcome utilities linearly with their probabilities. However, behavioral evidence shows that humans are systematically risk-sensitive, overweighting rare negative events and exhibiting loss aversion. We study the consequences of this mismatch in social robot navigation, where safety-critical outcomes (e.g., collisions) are rare but highly consequential. We compare EU with Cumulative Prospect Theory (CPT), a nonlinear model of human decision-making, within a Bradley-Terry preference learning framework. Our preliminary experiments show that when preferences are generated by risk-sensitive users, CPT-based learners recover reward functions with substantially lower regret compared to EU-based learners. Our results highlight the importance of modeling human risk sensitivity when learning rewards from preferences over stochastic robot outcomes.

</details>


### [86] [Environment Design for Reliable Shared Autonomy with Probabilistic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15487)
*Yi-Shiuan Tung,Himanshu Gupta,Gyanig Kumar,Heyang Huang,Bradley Hayes,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过优化工作空间布局来提升共享自主系统中人类意图推断的可靠性，并在仿真和真实实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自主系统大多在固定环境下优化意图推断，忽视了工作空间布局本身对推断难度的影响。本文旨在探索通过环境设计来提升推断可靠性这一被忽略的维度。

Method: 将工作空间布局形式化为优化问题，在有界噪声模型下推导概率正确性保证，通过仿真实验（多桌面场景）比较优化布局与基线布局，并在真实共享自主系统中集成该推断框架。

Result: 优化后的布局相比基线布局显著提高了目标推断可靠性并降低了歧义性，真实系统实验进一步验证了该方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 环境设计可作为提升共享自主系统的互补轴，通过优化物体物理排布来改善人机协作中的意图推断效果。

Abstract: Shared autonomy enables humans and robots to collaboratively perform tasks by combining human input with autonomous assistance. Most prior work focuses on improving intent inference under a fixed environment, overlooking how workspace design itself affects inference difficulty. We observe that the physical arrangement of objects directly influences the separability of candidate goals under noisy user inputs. We formulate workspace design as an optimization problem and derive a probabilistic correctness guarantee under a bounded noise model. Through simulation experiments across multiple tabletop scenarios, we show that optimized layouts improve goal inference reliability and reduce ambiguity compared to baseline arrangements. We further demonstrate a real-world shared autonomy system that integrates the proposed inference framework. This highlights the role of environment design as a complementary axis for improving shared autonomy systems.

</details>


### [87] [Multi-Objective Kinodynamic Motion Planning with Asymptotic Pareto Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15508)
*Yusif Razzaq,Anne Theurkauf,Nisar Ahmed,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SST算法的统一框架，通过在每个witness邻域维护局部Pareto最优节点集来解决多目标运动规划问题，涵盖字典序优化、约束优化和Pareto前沿近似三类问题，并提供了完备性和最优性的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本标量化方法无法在保证正确性的前提下扩展到连续域系统，亟需一种能处理运动动力学约束下多目标运动规划问题的统一算法框架。

Method: 基于Stable Sparse-RRT (SST)算法，将每个witness邻域维护的单一代表节点替换为局部Pareto最优节点集，衍生出三个变体：lexSST（字典序最小化）、coSST（约束优化）和poSST（Pareto前沿近似）。

Result: 对所提算法提供了完备性和最优性的理论证明，并通过大量实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架能够有效解决运动动力学约束下的多目标运动规划问题，为字典序、约束和Pareto前沿三类优化问题提供了具有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenge of multi-objective motion planning for systems under kinodynamic constraints. We consider three problem classes: (i) lexicographic optimization, in which objectives are minimized according to a strict priority ordering, (ii) constrained optimization, in which a primary objective is minimized subject to bounds on the remaining costs, and (iii) Pareto front optimization, in which the goal is to approximate the full set of optimal trade-offs among competing objectives. We first show that established cost scalarization methods for multi-objective problems cannot be extended to continuous-domain systems with correctness guarantees. Then, we propose a unified algorithmic framework built upon the Stable Sparse-RRT (SST) algorithm, in which the single representative maintained at each witness neighborhood is replaced by a representative set of locally Pareto-optimal nodes. This structure gives rise to three distinct algorithms: lexSST for lexicographic minimization, coSST for constrained optimization, and poSST for Pareto-front approximation. We provide theoretical guarantees for the completeness and optimality of our algorithms and demonstrate their effectiveness through extensive empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [88] [PACE: Persona Adaptation through Conversational Elicitation in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15579)
*Peizhen Li,Longbing Cao,Megani Rajendran,Timothy Liu,Aik Beng Ng,Simon See*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架，通过交互式对话引导在Ameca人形机器人上动态生成结构化、心理基础的人物角色，提升人机交互中的信任、拟人化感知和个性化体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖静态、硬编码的角色设定，缺乏适应个体用户上下文的灵活性，难以实现自然、投入且可信的人机交互。

Method: 提出PACE框架，包含交互式角色引导流水线（通过用户问答动态合成心理学基础的身份）、角色提示词编译阶段（生成多维度结构化提示词）以及具身系统集成（将结构化规范转化为多模态人形机器人行为）。

Result: 通过全面的人机交互实证评估，对比基线系统，评估动态生成角色在用户信任、拟人化感知、角色一致性、个人相关性和交互质量方面的影响。

Conclusion: 为具身人形助手部署个性化、可交互且可靠的角色身份建立了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Equipping humanoid robots with coherent and adaptable personas is crucial for fostering natural, engaging, and trustworthy human-robot interaction (HRI). However, existing approaches often rely on static, hard-coded identities that lack the flexibility to adapt to individual user contexts. In this paper, we present PACE (Persona Adaptation through Conversational Elicitation), a novel framework for the interactive generation and deployment of structured personas on the Ameca humanoid robot. Our system introduces an Interactive Persona Elicitation Pipeline, enabling the robot to dynamically synthesize a tailored, psychologically grounded identity through user Q&A. This elicitation process feeds into a persona prompt compilation phase, generating a structured persona prompt built upon multi-perspective dimensions. We detail the Embodied System Integration required to translate this structured specification into expressive, multimodal humanoid behaviors. Through a comprehensive empirical HRI evaluation, we assess the impact of dynamically generated personas on user trust, perceived anthropomorphism, persona consistency, personal relevance, and interaction quality compared to a generic baseline. These contributions establish a scalable pathway for deploying personalized, interactive, and reliable identities in embodied humanoid assistants. Video demo is available at: https://lipzh5.github.io/PACE/

</details>


### [89] [A Model-Based Decoupling Strategy for Proprioception and Contact Sensing in an Architected Soft Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15582)
*Francesco Stella,Annan Zhang,Cosimo Della Santina,Josie Hughes,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型驱动策略的软体连续体机器人，通过解耦本体感知和接触信号，利用稀疏流体压力传感器实现形状估计与接触检测。在单段结构上弯曲误差0.11±0.02，接触检测率97%；集成八段后实现了触觉示教、导纳控制和触觉物体重建等全身演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续体机器人需要嵌入式传感实现本体感知和接触检测，但在稀疏、高度可变的拱形结构中集成传感器仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出Innervated Trimmed Helicoid (ITH)结构，每段含六条空气通道呈局部锯齿形排列；利用过定系统配合分段常曲率模型映射压力到形状，采用Huber回归识别异常通道残差检测外部接触。

Result: 单段ITH形状估计弯曲误差0.11±0.02，178次试验中接触检测率97%；集成八段构成Air-Helix机械臂，实现触觉示教、力调节和物体重建。

Conclusion: 局部流体神经支配结合基于模型的冗余度解析是实现拱形软体机器人本体感知与接触检测共存的实用途径。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots require embedded sensing for proprioception and contact detection, yet integrating sensors into sparse, highly deformable architected structures remains challenging. We present a model-based strategy that decouples proprioceptive and contact signals from a common set of fluidic pressure sensors embedded in a soft architected segment. Each segment of the Innervated Trimmed Helicoid (ITH) contains six air channels routed in a localized zigzag pattern along the circumference. With only three principal kinematic degrees of freedom (axial compression, bending in x, bending in y), the six pressure readings form an overdetermined system. A piecewise constant curvature model maps pressures to shape, and Huber regression identifies outlier channels whose residuals indicate external contact. On a single ITH segment, this approach achieves proprioceptive shape estimation with a relative bending error of 0.11 +/- 0.02 and a contact detection rate of 97% across 178 trials. We integrate eight ITH segments into Air-Helix, a tendon-driven soft continuum manipulator, and present exploratory whole-arm demonstrations that include tactile teaching by demonstration, admittance-controlled force regulation, and tactile object reconstruction. The results suggest that localized fluidic innervation combined with model-based redundancy resolution is a practical path toward concurrent proprioception and contact sensing in architected soft robots.

</details>


### [90] [MemoGuard: An Adaptive Runtime for Guarding Against Memory Traps in Communication-Limited Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15589)
*Rajat Bhattacharjya,Hyeonjong Ju,Sing-Yao Wu,Eli Bozorgzadeh,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoGuard 是一个轻量级自适应运行时系统，通过拓扑、资源和结果契约验证来检测和避免"记忆陷阱"（高相似度但执行无效的 episodic memories），在降低电池安全违规的同时减少本地推理回退开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信受限的机器人在关键任务场景中需要可靠的机载决策。episodic memory 复用是一种低成本的回退方案，但检索相似性不能保证执行有效性——高相似度的动作可能因拓扑变化、电池余量不足或先前结果不可靠而变得不安全。这种"记忆陷阱"在安全性与效率之间产生了设计空间：纯相似性复用成本低但不安全，始终使用本地推理则计算和能耗成本高。

Method: MemoGuard 提出了一种轻量级自适应运行时框架，在复用 episodic memories 之前，对它们进行三重契约验证：(1) 拓扑契约——检查环境拓扑是否与记忆中的一致；(2) 资源契约——检查电池等资源余量是否充足；(3) 结果契约——检查先前执行结果是否可靠。仅当验证通过时才复用记忆，否则回退到本地推理。

Result: 在基于图的走廊巡检模拟器中，与纯相似性 Top-1 复用相比，MemoGuard 将电池安全违规减少了 76.6%；与始终使用推理相比，将回退调用减少了 21.4%。在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier 上使用本地 llama3.2:3b 作为回退推理模型时，每次试验避免了 3.67 秒和 36.97 焦耳的回退推理开销。

Conclusion: MemoGuard 通过在复用前引入轻量级契约验证，有效平衡了 episodic memory 复用的安全性与效率，在显著降低安全违规的同时减少了计算和能耗开销，适用于通信受限的机器人任务场景。

Abstract: Communication-limited robots in mission-critical scenarios such as disaster inspection and search-and-rescue must make reliable onboard decisions without access to remote operators or high-capacity reasoning services. Episodic memory reuse is an attractive low-cost fallback, but retrieval similarity does not guarantee execution validity, i.e., a retrieved action may match the current context yet be unsafe due to changed topology, insufficient battery margin, or unreliable prior outcomes. We call such high-similarity but execution-invalid episodes memory traps. This creates a safety-efficiency design space where similarity only reuse minimizes fallback cost but can be unsafe, while always invoking local reasoning improves safety at high computational and energy cost. This paper presents MemoGuard, a lightweight adaptive runtime that validates episodic memories against topology, resource, and outcome contracts before reuse, invoking fallback only when validation fails. In a graph-based corridor-inspection simulator, MemoGuard reduces battery safety violations by 76.6% over similarity-only top-1 reuse while reducing fallback calls by 21.4% over always reasoning. On an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier with local llama3.2:3b fallback reasoning, this corresponds to 3.67 s and 36.97 J of avoided fallback-reasoning overhead per trial. We open-source MemoGuard at https://github.com/hetheiin/memoguard.

</details>


### [91] [AEGIS: Assay-Aware Protocol Validation and Runtime Monitoring for Open-Source Liquid Handling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15620)
*Priyanka V. Setty,Arvind Ramanathan,Ian Foster,Rick Stevens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEGIS是一个针对Opentrons OT-2液体处理平台的双层防护系统：第一层通过LLM+规则数据库检测协议中的逻辑错误（F1=0.97），第二层利用PCA世界模型结合YOLO视觉检测运行时物理故障（AP=0.89），实现了开源的、免API费用的预飞实验验证与实时视觉监控统一方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本液体处理器（如Opentrons OT-2）缺少压力监控，存在两类未检测的故障模式：语法正确但违反实验规则的协议逻辑错误（如移液枪头在PCR模板与无模板对照间重复使用），以及运行时物理执行故障（部分分液、气泡、漏装枪头），现有系统开环运行无法检测这些问题。

Method: 提出AEGIS双层守护架构：第一层将人工整理的机器可读实验规则数据库与LLM推理引擎结合，分析OT-2 Python代码；第二层使用PCA世界模型拟合经YOLO裁剪的四帧移液器轨迹，并引入始终开启的VLM自投票门控机制级联处理。

Result: 第一层在5个实验家族24个协议基准上达到调整F1=0.97，开源模型与最优闭源模型持平；第二层在留一板评估下AP=0.89、F1=0.71（AUROC=0.80），p20小型移液器分辨率极限F1=0.47。实物演示中确定性捕获漏装枪头故障，部分分液召回率提升至5/5，级联处理将VLM成本降至每板$1.63对比基线$10.33。

Conclusion: AEGIS是首个为开源液体处理器统一预飞实验感知验证与运行时视觉监控的系统，开源发布，透明水样检测的局限性通过低置信度VLM推理暴露而非错误判定。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories increasingly rely on low-cost liquid handlers such as the Opentrons OT-2, which ship without the pressure-based aspiration monitoring of Hamilton or Tecan systems and are typically run open-loop. Two failure modes go undetected: protocols that are syntactically valid but violate assay-specific invariants (e.g., tip reuse between a PCR template and a no-template control), and physical execution failures (partial dispense, air bubbles, missing tips) at runtime. We present AEGIS, a two-layer guardian for both. Layer 1 pairs a curated machine-readable assay rule database with an LLM that reasons over OT-2 Python code, reaching an adjusted F1 of 0.97 on a 24-protocol benchmark across five assay families and beating rules-only and LLM-only ablations across five backends; a free open-weight model ties the best proprietary one, so no paid API is required. Layer 2 fits a PCA world model to YOLO-cropped four-frame pipette trajectories; under a leakage-free leave-one-plate-out evaluation it reaches average precision 0.89 and operating-point F1 0.71 (AUROC 0.80), a deployment-faithful number that matches the live demonstration, and we characterize the small-pipette (p20) resolution limit (F1 0.47). A live demonstration on a physical OT-2 (five replicates per condition) catches planted no-tip failures deterministically and partial dispense on coloured dyes, with an always-VLM self-vote gate lifting partial-dispense recall to 5/5; transparent water is a principled limit of any front-view-only monitor, which AEGIS surfaces as low-confidence VLM reasoning rather than a wrong verdict. Cascade triage holds VLM cost near $1.63 per plate versus $10.33 for an always-VLM baseline. AEGIS is open source and, to our knowledge, the first system to unify pre-flight assay-aware validation with runtime visual monitoring for an open-source liquid handler.

</details>


### [92] [Think at 5 Hz, Act at 20 Hz: Asynchronous Fast-Slow Vision-Language-Action Inference for Closed-Loop Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15621)
*Yun Li,Jiachen Gong,Simon Thompson,Ehsan Javanmardi,Qunli Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Shiming Liu,Peng Wang,Zixuan Guo,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种快慢架构，将冻结的7B视觉语言模型作为慢系统（低频处理导航指令和视觉历史，暴露每层KV缓存作为场景表征），轻量级动作专家作为快系统（每模拟步长50ms内通过注意力机制处理当前帧和缓存回归路点），通过随机陈旧性训练对齐异步执行，在CARLA LangAuto-Short路线上将路线完成率从37.0提升至94.0，红灯违规减少三分之一，零样本迁移到未见城镇仍保持84-94%完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在端到端驾驶中带来指令跟随和场景推理能力，但其推理延迟与车辆所需的控制速率存在冲突。现有闭环智能体通过交替模拟步长调用模型并回放上一命令来掩盖这一差距，导致一半的控制输出忽略最新观测。

Method: 1) 冻结7B视觉语言骨干作为慢系统，低频处理导航指令和视觉历史，暴露每层KV缓存作为场景表征；2) 轻量级动作专家作为快系统，每模拟步长通过注意力机制处理当前相机帧和缓存，单次前向回归路点；3) 在随机陈旧性条件下训练专家，对齐训练与异步执行时的时延分布。

Result: 1) 每50ms产生新鲜控制，路线完成率从37.0提升至94.0；2) 帧跳过消融表明专家本身提升驾驶分数，而逐步长新鲜度将完成率从82.1提升至94.0，红灯违规减少三分之一；3) 零样本迁移到两个未见城镇保持84-94%路线完成率（基线31-41%）；4) 开放环路点误差较骨干自身动作头降低近四倍，单消费级GPU上每步长模型成本32ms且与历史长度无关。

Conclusion: 快慢架构有效解决了LLM推理延迟与实时控制需求的矛盾，通过解耦高频动作执行与低频场景理解，在不牺牲指令跟随和场景推理能力的前提下实现了实时闭环驾驶，且具有良好的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models bring instruction following and scene reasoning to end-to-end driving, but their inference latency collides with the control rate a vehicle requires. Existing closed-loop agents hide this gap by invoking the model on alternate simulation ticks and replaying the previous command in between, so half of all control outputs ignore the newest observations. We present a fast-slow architecture that removes this compromise. A frozen 7B vision-language backbone acts as the slow system, digesting navigation instructions and visual history at low frequency while exposing its per-layer key-value cache as a standing representation of the scene. A lightweight action expert acts as the fast system, attending to this cache and to the current camera frame at every simulation tick to regress waypoints in a single forward pass. Since the cache lags behind the world at deployment, we train the expert under randomized staleness, aligning training with asynchronous execution. On LangAuto-Short routes in CARLA, our system produces fresh control at every 50 ms simulation tick and lifts route completion from 37.0 to 94.0 over the frame-skipping baseline. A frame-skip ablation with the same expert separates the two factors at work: the expert raises the driving score on its own, while per-tick freshness raises completion from 82.1 to 94.0 and cuts red-light violations by a third. Trained on a single town, the expert transfers zero-shot to two unseen towns, holding 84-94% route completion where the baseline reaches 31-41%. It reduces open-loop waypoint error by nearly a factor of four compared to the backbone's own action head, at a per-tick model cost of 32 ms that is independent of history length on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [93] [Scalable Open-Source Visuotactile Sensor for 6-Axis Contact Wrench Estimation in Tensegrity Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15633)
*Wenzhe Tong,Jonathan Mi,Xili Yi,Nima Fazeli,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展、开源的视触觉传感系统，专门用于张拉整体机器人。该系统通过端盖传感器集成弹性外壳、3D打印TPU接口和嵌入式相机，采用新型螺旋填充粘合技术实现无胶粘接，能够实现六轴力/力矩估计和接触检测。实验表明，该系统在静态验证数据上MSE达0.1531，并成功应用于12kg张拉整体机器人的地面接触识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人缺乏实用的触觉反馈机制，现有触觉传感方案成本高、可重复性差，难以大规模集成。本文旨在开发一种低成本、可复现、物理可解释的触觉感知方案，实现接触感知的本体感觉与状态估计。

Method: 设计了一种端盖传感器，包含弹性外壳、3D打印TPU接口和嵌入相机及LED照明环的刚性底座。采用新型螺旋填充粘合技术实现弹性体-TPU界面的无胶粘接。通过触觉到力/力矩神经网络，将剪切矢量场映射到六维力和力矩测量值。

Result: 静态验证数据上MSE为0.1531，具备动态运动下的域外泛化能力。在12kg张拉整体机器人上成功实现地面接触识别，验证了全系统集成效果。

Conclusion: 该系统显著提升了张拉整体机器人触觉反馈的实用性，提供了一种低成本、可复现、物理可解释的路径，可用于接触感知的本体感觉和状态估计。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable, open-source visuotactile sensing system for tensegrity robots that enables six-axis wrench estimation and contact detection. The proposed endcap sensor integrates an elastomeric shell, a 3D-printed thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU) interface, and a rigid base housing an embedded camera and LED illumination ring. A novel gyroid-infill bonding technique is introduced to form a durable elastomer-TPU interface without adhesives, yielding a lightweight and modular design compatible with large-scale tensegrity structures. A tactile-to-wrench neural network maps shear vector fields to six-dimensional force and torque measurements. Experimental results demonstrate accurate and stable wrench estimation with a mean squared error (MSE) of 0.1531 on static validation data and out-of-domain generalization under dynamic motion. Furthermore, full-system integration on a 12 kg tensegrity robot confirms the sensor's ability to reliably identify ground contacts. The system substantially improves the practicality of tactile feedback for tensegrity robots, offering a low-cost, reproducible, and physically interpretable pathway toward contact-aware proprioception and state estimation. Open source files are available at \href{https://github.com/Jonathan-Twz/tensegrity-gelfoot}{github.com/Jonathan-Twz/tensegrity-gelfoot}

</details>


### [94] [IMBench: A Benchmark for Intuitive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15641)
*Anurag Maurya,Sukhvansh Jain,Prajwal Avhad,Gautham Balachandran,Ziyi Zhou,Atharva Kshirsagar,Satyam Singh,Bowen Li. Rishabh Mukund,Ritul Singh,Jatin Vira,Suvonil Chatterjee,Devesh K. Jha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMBENCH是一个评估直觉操控能力的基准，涵盖感知、物理推理、动作生成与迭代执行，35个任务、14K轨迹的实验表明现有VLM和VLA模型在整合推理与执行方面存在明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么孤立评估物理推理而不考虑执行，要么只衡量策略性能而不要求显式推理，缺乏对直觉操控这一将推理与运动控制整合的能力的综合评估。

Method: 构建包含35个任务、14K过滤轨迹的基准IMBENCH，要求模型在接触丰富的操控、工具使用、多阶段依赖等约束下推断物理结构并生成可行动作序列，提供可扩展的场景生成工具。

Result: 视觉语言模型具备部分物理推理能力但无法生成可执行计划，视觉-语言-动作模型难以满足任务约束且泛化能力差，两类模型均存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 直觉操控是当前基础模型和通用机器人策略缺失的关键能力维度，IMBENCH为评估和推动更整合的自适应物理智能提供了基准。

Abstract: Humans combine reasoning and motor control to solve complex manipulation tasks under diverse constraints. They build an understanding of the physical world that helps them convert reasoning into actions and quickly adapt to new scenes, tasks, and rules. We refer to this capability as intuitive manipulation. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this integration: they evaluate physical reasoning in isolation from execution, or measure policy performance without requiring explicit reasoning. We introduce IMBENCH, a benchmark designed to evaluate intuitive manipulation as an integrated capability spanning perception, physical reasoning, action generation, and iterative execution. Our tasks require models to infer task-relevant physical structure and generate feasible action sequences under explicit constraints, including contact-rich manipulation, tool use, and multi-stage dependencies. We introduce a benchmark of 35 tasks, 14K filtered trajectories, and scalable tools for generating diverse scenarios. Experiments reveal a consistent gap: vision language models show partial physical reasoning ability but fail to produce executable plans, while state-of-the-art vision-language-action models struggle to satisfy task constraints and generalize across scenarios. These results identify intuitive manipulation as a missing axis in current foundation models and generalist robot policies, and position IMBENCH as a step toward evaluating and enabling more integrated, adaptive physical intelligence.

</details>


### [95] [Difference-Based Relational Learning for Zero-Shot Object-Goal Visual Navigation With Direct Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15642)
*Guolei Qi,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出T-DRN网络，通过Siamese差异特征提取器和双帧时间缓冲区，实现零样本从仿真到真实环境的物体目标视觉导航，在AI2-THOR仿真和实体轮式机器人上均验证了其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端深度强化学习在零样本物体目标视觉导航中面临仿真到真实的差距，尤其是物体外观变化和受限的相机视场角问题。

Method: 提出T-DRN，包含两个核心组件：1）Siamese差异特征提取器，计算目标与观测物体之间的关系差异，生成域无关表示；2）双帧时间缓冲区，在窄视场下保持短期物体连续性。

Result: 在AI2-THOR仿真环境中，T-DRN在零样本泛化的成功率上优于强基线方法；在实体轮式机器人上同样展现了鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: T-DRN支持直接从仿真到真实环境的迁移，在真实感知和运动约束下仍能保持鲁棒表现。

Abstract: End-to-end deep reinforcement learning (DRL) for zero-shot object-goal visual navigation remains challenged by the sim-to-real gap, particularly variations in object appearance and restricted camera field-of-view (FoV). This letter proposes a Temporal Difference-Relational Network (T-DRN) for robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. T-DRN combines a Siamese difference-based feature extractor, which computes relational difference between the target and observed objects to produce domain-independent representations, with a dual-frame temporal buffer that preserves short-term object continuity under narrow FoV. Extensive experiments in AI2-THOR demonstrate that T-DRN improves zero-shot generalization in terms of success rates over strong baselines. Furthermore, T-DRN is systematically validated on a physical wheeled robot, demonstrating robust performance under real sensing and actuation constraints and supporting the feasibility of direct sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [96] [Learning a System-Level Surrogate for Hydraulic Excavators: A Simulation-to-Real LSTM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15656)
*Shuai Wang,Shen Wang,Qiang Wang,Muguo Du,Donghai Shi,Chenyu Wang,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LSTM的仿真到现实框架，将液压挖掘机视为输入-输出算子，学习系统级数字代理模型，并引入自适应卡尔曼滤波处理真实数据不一致性，实现高保真的角速度和长时轨迹复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主液压挖掘机的开发受限于物理机器获取困难及真实实验成本高，亟需一种可扩展、高效的自动化算法开发方法。

Method: 采用LSTM网络将挖掘机视为输入-输出算子，学习其闭环行为数字代理；先在MuJoCo仿真环境验证，再迁移至真实挖掘机；引入基于自适应卡尔曼滤波的一致性感知状态估计方法处理真实数据测量不一致性。

Result: 在闭环自回归评估下，所学代理模型在角速度和长时轨迹复现上均实现高保真度。

Conclusion: 所提模型可作为仿真与物理系统的即插即用代理，实现挖掘机自动化算法的可扩展高效开发。

Abstract: Developing autonomous hydraulic excavators is constrained by limited access to physical machines and the high cost of real-world experimentation. This paper proposes a simulation-to-real framework for learning a system-level digital surrogate using Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. Instead of modeling internal dynamics, the excavator is treated as an input-output operator, and the surrogate is trained to reproduce its closed-loop behavior under identical control inputs. The approach is first validated in a MuJoCo simulation environment and then transferred to a real excavator. To address measurement inconsistencies in real-world data, a consistency-aware state estimation method based on adaptive Kalman filtering is introduced. Experimental results demonstrate that the learned surrogate achieves high fidelity in both angular velocity and long-horizon trajectory reproduction under closed-loop autoregressive evaluation. These results confirm that the proposed model can serve as a drop-in surrogate for both simulation and physical systems, enabling scalable and efficient development of excavation automation algorithms.

</details>


### [97] [Continuously Stable Structure through Plastic Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15659)
*Junlong Xiao,Yaoqiang Pan,Xuan Zhang,Michael Yu Wang,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于塑性变形的连续稳定结构（CSSPD），将其集成到软体夹爪中。通过塑性变形机制，夹爪无需能量输入即可维持连续构型，增加的刚度保证了静态和动态稳定性。实验表明，夹爪可无能量输入承受高达16 N的被动保持力，性能媲美0.3 MPa气动驱动；在气动驱动结合下，可在高达400 m/s²的脉冲加速度下保持稳定，并能无电源长时间被动栖息在树枝上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人虽具有顺应性和适应性优势，但在动态环境中稳定性不足，尤其软体夹爪在加速度或外部干扰下易导致抓取失败。本研究旨在解决软体夹爪的稳定性问题。

Method: 提出CSSPD结构（连续稳定结构通过塑性变形），集成塑性变形机制使夹爪无需能量输入维持构型并增加刚度；引入仿生爪垫增强稳定性并实现基于感知的快速物体抓取；建立数学模型并优化金属层的kirigami结构。

Result: 夹爪无能量输入可承受高达16 N被动保持力（性能媲美0.3 MPa气动驱动）；结合气动驱动时可在400 m/s²脉冲加速度下保持稳定；可无电源长时间被动栖息于树枝上。

Conclusion: CSSPD结构有效解决了软体夹爪的稳定性问题，在移动机器人应用中展现出良好前景。

Abstract: Soft robots have seen widespread adoption in interactive tasks due to their inherent compliance and adaptability. However, these advantages often come at the cost of stability, posing challenges in a dynamic environment. This limitation is especially critical in soft grippers, where instability under acceleration or external disturbances can result in grasp failure. In this study, we present a continuously stable structure through plastic deformation (CSSPD), integrated into a soft gripper. By leveraging the mechanism of plastic deformation, the gripper maintains continuous configurations without energy input, while the added stiffness ensures both static and dynamic stability. We introduce a bioinspired paw pad that significantly enhances stability and enables sensing-based rapid object grasping. Then we develop the mathematical model and optimize the kirigami structure of the metal layer. Experimental results show that the gripper can sustain a passive holding force of up to 16 N without energy input, achieving performance comparable to pneumatic actuation at 0.3 MPa. When combined with pneumatic actuation, it remains stable under pulsed accelerations of up to 400 m/s^2. It can also passively perch on tree branches for extended periods without power, demonstrating promise for mobile robotic applications.

</details>


### [98] [A Generative Partially Specified Finite State Machine Approach to Complex Behaviour Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15674)
*Kalana Ratnayake,Michael Pritchard,David Hinwood,Maleen Jayasuriya,Damith Herath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式部分指定有限状态机（GPSFSM）神经符号架构，首次将FSM用于机器人行为规划的生成式方法。通过Fabric引擎、Capabilities2异步事件系统和PromptTools统一LLM接口，在导航任务中实现了比BTGenBot更高的规划生成成功率，尤其在零样本场景中表现优异，并开源了完整的ROS2实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型已成功集成行为树（BT）用于动态重规划，但有限状态机（FSM）因其计算效率高且被广泛采用，却尚未被探索用于生成式方法。作者希望填补这一空白，利用FSM的符号化语义结构实现行为规划。

Method: 提出GPSFSM神经符号架构，包含三个关键组件：Fabric引擎（解析、验证和执行含Sequential/Recovery/Parallel-Any/Parallel-All控制结构的FSM）、Capabilities2（在ROS2中引入异步事件系统用于行为链式调用和运行时参数注入）、PromptTools（统一本地与云端LLM接口，支持提示缓冲和动态异步组合）。

Result: 在导航任务中，GPSFSM的规划生成成功率持续优于SOTA系统BTGenBot，尤其在零样本场景中表现突出（BT通常在此场景表现不佳），同时保持与前沿LLM相当或更低的规划延迟。系统还能生成复杂行为。

Conclusion: 首次将生成式FSM引入机器人行为规划，证明了FSM在生成式方法中的可行性和优势，开源ROS2栈使生成式FSM规划变得实用和可复现。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in dynamic environments require behaviour planning systems that combine reactivity, interpretability, and adaptability. While Large Language Models have been successfully integrated with Behaviour Trees for dynamic replanning, Finite State Machines, despite their widespread adoption and computational efficiency, remain unexplored for generative approaches. We propose a Generative Partially Specified Finite State Machine (GPSFSM) neurosymbolic architecture that utilises the symbolic and semantic structure of FSMs, including states and event-triggered transitions, to implement Behaviour Planning. This paper introduces the first GPSFSM framework for robotics, featuring Fabric, an FSM engine that parses, validates, and executes behaviour plans that contain Sequential, Recovery, Parallel-Any, and Parallel-All control structures. We extend the Capabilities2 package in ROS2 with an asynchronous event system for behaviour chaining and runtime parameter injection for configurable execution, addressing the ad-hoc function representations that limit current generative systems. PromptTools provides a unified ROS 2 interface to local and cloud LLMs, with prompt buffering, enabling dynamic asynchronous composition of task and context information. Together, these components enable standardised semantic capability descriptions for robot-agnostic development. Experimental evaluation on navigation tasks demonstrates that our GPSFSM approach achieves consistently higher plan-generation success rates than the state-of-the-art BTGenBot system, particularly excelling in zero-shot scenarios where BTs typically struggle, while maintaining comparable or lower planning latency to frontier LLMs. We also demonstrate that our system can generate complex behaviours. We release an open-source ROS2 stack that makes generative FSM planning practical and reproducible for robotic systems.

</details>


### [99] [RAVEN: Reinforcement-Adaptive Visibility-Graph Planning for Robust Humanoid Navigation with Collision-Free MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15701)
*Ruochen Hou,Shiqi Wang,Beom Jun Kim,Hanzhang Fang,Mehak Singal,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEN提出了一种分层强化学习-模型预测控制框架，通过RL自适应调整可见性图规划器的几何参数（如障碍物膨胀），结合约束MPC跟踪，提升人形机器人在动态环境中的导航鲁棒性，相比端到端学习和手动调参基线减少了超调并提高了窄通道通行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可见性图规划器结合MPC在理论上能生成无碰撞轨迹，但实际系统中控制延迟、状态估计噪声和 locomotion 不确定性会导致超调与约束违反，而手动调参和精确系统建模难以应对这些不确定性。

Method: 采用分层架构：上层通过RL学习调整可见性图规划器的障碍物膨胀半径等几何参数，重塑自由空间拓扑以补偿延迟和跟踪误差；下层采用带碰撞约束的MPC跟踪规划轨迹，显式强制执行速度边界和避障约束。

Result: 在真实延迟和观测噪声下，RAVEN相比手动调参的可见性图-MPC基线和纯RL导航策略，减少了障碍物附近的超调，提高了窄通道导航的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 自适应图构造与约束MPC结合提供了一种有效且可解释的鲁棒人形导航替代方案，优于端到端学习方法。

Abstract: Humanoid navigation in dynamic environments requires long-horizon planning while respecting short-horizon dynamic and safety constraints. Classical visibility-graph planners combined with model predictive control (MPC) can efficiently generate collision-free trajectories, but their performance depends on manually tuned parameters and accurate system modeling. In real robotic systems, control delays, state-estimation noise, and locomotion uncertainties can cause overshoot and constraint violations even when the nominal path is geometrically optimal. We propose RAVEN, a hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL)-MPC framework for robust humanoid navigation. Unlike prior approaches that use learning to tune cost weights or replace planning entirely, RAVEN employs RL to adapt the geometric construction of a visibility-graph planner by modifying obstacle inflation and related graph parameters. By directly reshaping the free-space geometry, the learned planner alters the topology of the global path to compensate for delay and tracking imperfections. A collision-free MPC layer then tracks the planned trajectory while explicitly enforcing velocity bounds and obstacle-avoidance constraints. By training under realistic delays and observation noise, RAVEN learns planning adaptations that improve robustness while retaining explicit long-horizon geometric planning and constrained optimization, in contrast to end-to-end learning approaches. We evaluate RAVEN against a manually tuned visibility-graph MPC baseline and a pure RL navigation policy. Results demonstrate reduced overshoot near obstacles, improved robustness in narrow passages, and more reliable navigation under delay and noise. These findings indicate that reinforcement-adaptive graph construction combined with constrained MPC provides an effective and interpretable alternative to end-to-end learning for robust humanoid navigation.

</details>


### [100] [Implicit Virtual Leader: Decentralized Vision-Only Relative Pose Estimation for Multi-Robot Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15708)
*Shiyuan Yang,Zelin Wang,Zhijia Tao,Yilin Wang,Zhengyu Hou,Xiaosong Kong,Borong Zhang,Yip Fun Yeung,Yuankai Luo,Sharon Lee,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图神经网络(GNN)的完全去中心化视觉相对位姿估计框架，通过隐式虚拟领导者(IVL)概念，仅使用单目图像和机器人间通信实现无GPS环境下的编队控制，并集成了异方差高斯负对数似然(GNLL)头和MC Dropout进行不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典领航-跟随者编队控制存在单点故障、误差传播问题，且依赖绝对定位传感器，不适合GPS拒止环境。

Method: 提出隐式虚拟领导者(IVL)概念作为非物理编队参考系，通过GNN隐式学习；采用异方差GNLL头量化偶然不确定性，MC Dropout量化认知不确定性。

Result: 在仿真和真实测试集上取得了有竞争力的位姿估计精度，并自然泛化到异构机器人平台和不同编队规模。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效克服了传统方法的局限性，实现了去中心化、仅依赖视觉的相对位姿估计，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Classical leader-follower formation control suffers from single points of failure and error propagation, and relies on absolute localization sensors that are ill-suited for GPS-denied environments. We address these limitations by introducing a fully decentralized, vision-only relative pose estimation framework based on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). The key idea is the implicit virtual leader (IVL): a non-physical formation reference frame that is not tied to any individual robot but is implicitly learned within the GNN using only monocular images and inter-robot communication. We attach a heteroscedastic GNLL head for aleatoric uncertainty and MC~Dropout for epistemic uncertainty, and conduct a systematic comparison across simulation and real-world test sets. Our framework achieves competitive pose estimation accuracy and generalizes naturally to heterogeneous robot platforms and varying formation sizes.

</details>


### [101] [AC-VLA: Robust Out-of-Distribution Action Execution via Compositional Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15714)
*Xiaojiang Peng,Kai Peng,Jie Lu,Zheng Lian,Zitong YU,Xiaobo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AC-VLA框架通过组合学习模块（LLM指令分解+本体轨迹对齐）和状态条件非对称掩码策略，解决了VLA模型在子任务重组场景下的轨迹过拟合与感知捷径问题，在LIBERO-OOD任务上实现约28%的绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在端到端机器人操作中表现优异，但当熟悉子任务以未见方式重新组合时，面临分布外泛化困难。现有模型存在轨迹过拟合（过度拟合整体轨迹模式而非组合性子技能语义）和感知捷径（动作令牌过度依赖腕部视角纹理而忽略全局空间定位）两种相互强化的失败模式。

Method: 提出即插即用的AC-VLA框架，包含两个架构无关组件：(1)组合学习模块——利用LLM驱动的指令分解器和本体轨迹对齐器生成密集的子任务监督，通过完整演示与分解数据的混合训练赋予模型组合泛化能力；(2)状态条件非对称掩码策略——在夹爪闭合阶段抑制腕部视角输入，强制模型依赖全局语义定位。所有组件无需修改架构，可直接集成到任何VLA骨干网络。

Result: 在π_{0.5}模型上实例化，并在LIBERO和LIBERO-OOD基准上评估，AC-VLA在组合性OOD任务上实现约28%的绝对提升，同时保持近乎完美的分布内性能。

Conclusion: AC-VLA通过组合学习与感知正则化双管齐下，有效解决了VLA模型在子任务重组的OOD场景下的两大失败模式，且作为插件式方法具备良好的通用性与兼容性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at end-to-end robotic manipulation but struggle with out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization when familiar sub-tasks are recombined in unseen configurations. We identify two mutually reinforcing failure modes: \emph{trajectory overfitting}, where models overfit to holistic trajectory patterns rather than compositional sub-skill semantics; and \emph{perceptual shortcut}, where action tokens over-rely on wrist-view textures at the expense of global spatial grounding. To address both, we introduce \textbf{AC-VLA}, a plug-and-play Action Compositional learning framework comprising two architecture-agnostic components: \textbf{(i)} a compositional learning module that uses an LLM-driven instruction decomposer and a proprioceptive trajectory aligner to generate dense sub-task supervision, followed by mixed training on complete demonstrations and decomposed data to endow the model with compositional generalization; and \textbf{(ii)} a state-conditioned asymmetric masking strategy that suppresses wrist-view inputs during closed-gripper phases, enforcing global semantic grounding. All components are architectural modification-free and directly integrable into any VLA backbone. Instantiated on $π_{0.5}$ and evaluated on LIBERO and LIBERO-OOD benchmarks, AC-VLA achieves a ~28% absolute improvement on compositional OOD tasks while maintaining near-perfect in-distribution performance.

</details>


### [102] [A Task-Space Receding Horizon Controller for Fast Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15733)
*Mattia Penzotti,Marco Controzzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务空间滚动时域控制器，通过短时接触一致的滚转生成终端运动学参考，仅计算朝向该参考的平滑最小加速度过渡的第一个输入，在动态杂乱环境中实现了兼顾前瞻性、响应速度与计算效率的实时避障控制，并在40-DOF多链系统仿真和6-DOF平台硬件实验中验证了其优于动态优化织物和MPC基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模型预测控制（MPC）虽能提供前瞻性，但随预测时域、模型复杂度及几何约束数量增加，在线计算成本迅速增长；而无时域的反应式方法计算高效，但在动态杂乱环境中缺乏远见，容易陷入局部困境。本文旨在兼顾前瞻性与实时性，提出一种计算高效且具有前瞻能力的避障控制方法。

Method: 提出任务空间滚动时域控制器：首先通过短时接触一致的滚转（使用膨胀凸几何体的迭代动力学求解器）生成满足内部非穿透约束的终端运动学参考；然后仅计算从当前状态朝向该参考的平滑最小加速度过渡的第一个控制输入。该滚转基于闭环逆运动学调节律，可融合机器人-障碍物接触、动态障碍物运动及自碰撞信息，无需完整约束轨迹优化。

Result: 在40-DOF多链系统仿真中验证了中等预测时域能在前瞻性、响应速度与计算成本之间取得平衡；在6-DOF平台硬件实验中展现了一致的仿真到现实迁移行为，无需精确惯性参数估计；与动态优化织物和MPC基线的对比表明，在动态杂乱环境中提高了任务成功率，同时保持了解算时间在实时执行可接受的范围内。

Conclusion: 本文方法在接触非活跃的闭环下证明了局部指数级任务空间调节能力；对于滚转内部激活的接触，刻画了相应的离散更新并界定了移动障碍物对正则工作集的影响。总体上，该控制器在计算效率和前瞻能力之间取得了有效折中，适用于实时机器人避障控制。

Abstract: Real-time collision avoidance for robotic manipulators requires fast reactions to unexpected obstacle motion and lookahead to avoid becoming trapped by near-future constraints. Full model predictive control can provide this foresight, but its online cost may grow quickly with horizon length, model fidelity, and the number of active geometric constraints. Conversely, horizon-free reactive methods are computationally efficient but can be short-sighted in dynamic clutter. We present a task-space receding-horizon controller that uses a short contact-consistent rollout to generate a terminal kinematic reference satisfying internal non-penetration constraints, then computes only the first input of a smooth minimum-acceleration transition toward that reference. Starting from a closed-loop inverse-kinematics regulation law, the rollout is performed with an iterative dynamics solver operating on inflated convex robot and obstacle geometries, so that robot-obstacle contacts, dynamic obstacle motion, and self-collisions can shape the terminal reference without requiring full constrained trajectory optimization. We analyze the contact-inactive closed loop and show local exponential task-space regulation under standard regularity assumptions. For contacts activated inside the rollout, we characterize the corresponding discrete updates and bound the effect of moving obstacles on regular operating sets. Simulations on a 40-DOF multi-chain system show that intermediate horizons balance anticipation, responsiveness, and computational cost. Hardware experiments on a 6-DOF platform demonstrate consistent sim-to-real behavior without accurate inertial parameter estimation, and comparisons against dynamic optimization fabrics and model predictive control (MPC) baselines show improved success rates in dynamic clutter while preserving solve times compatible with real-time execution in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [103] [Towards Artificial Nerves: Biomimetic Optical-Fiber Tactile Sensing for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15746)
*Laura E. Butcher,Chris J. Ford,Nathan F. Lepora,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OptiTac是一种仿生触觉传感器，通过将每个机械引脚与充当人工神经的光纤配对，模拟人类触觉中从机械感受器到神经的结构，实现高空间分辨率的分布式触觉感知，并使用简单的解析方法（而非深度学习）推断接触位置、大小和形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需要接近人类皮肤的适应性和分辨率的触觉感知能力，以实现灵巧操作和安全交互，但现有技术在分布式触觉感知方面存在局限性。

Method: 设计了仿生触觉传感器OptiTac，采用软皮肤上的机械引脚与光纤配对的结构，将触觉信息从传感表面引出，将触觉信号视为图像，使用简单的解析方法推断接触信息。

Result: 实现了高空间分辨率的分布式触觉感知，能够通过可解释的解析方法推断接触位置、大小和形状，无需依赖黑箱深度学习模型。

Conclusion: OptiTac展示了生物学进化原理可指导机器人人工神经系统的开发，为下一代机器人平台实现类人触觉感知提供了可扩展的分布式触觉系统路径。

Abstract: Robotic systems increasingly demand tactile sensing that approaches the adaptability and resolution of human skin to enable dexterous manipulation and safe interaction. OptiTac is a biomimetic tactile sensor that emulates the mechanoreceptor-to-nerve architecture of human touch by pairing each mechanical pin on a soft skin with an optical fiber acting as an artificial nerve. This design demonstrates an architectural principle for routing tactile information away from the sensing surface while preserving high spatial resolution, establishing a practical route toward distributed tactile sensing in future robotic systems. By treating tactile signals as images, simple analytical methods, rather than opaque deep-learning models, are used to infer contact location, size, and shape, providing interpretable and scalable tactile intelligence. This work demonstrates how evolutionary principles from biology can guide the development of artificial nerve systems for robots, offering a pathway toward human-like tactile perception in next-generation robotic platforms. More broadly, OptiTac establishes an artificial nerve-inspired sensing framework for interpretable robotic touch and a scalable route toward future distributed tactile systems.

</details>


### [104] [SkillNav: Score-Level Skill Intervention for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15758)
*Ruijie Sang,Yiqun Duan,Pinhan Fu,Ruilin Wang,Wei Sui,Xianda Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillNav提出了一种可扩展的行为技能框架，通过在VLM导航器已有的好奇心价值图上以零令牌成本写入行为记忆，将技能分为三级（软缩放、下限提升、硬覆盖）并按固定顺序协作，实现了无需训练的零样本目标导航新SOTA（MP3D SPL 25.5, HM3D v0.1 SPL 39.3/SR 69.7, HM3D v0.2 SPL 43.2/SR 75.9）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM导航智能体仅依赖单帧推理，缺乏跨步骤的行为感知能力，导致死胡同卡顿、室内循环、目标迂回等重复性失败。基于提示词的补救措施不仅增加令牌预算，且难以编码角度、地图单元、视点坐标等空间信号。

Method: SkillNav框架将好奇心价值图作为可写入基底，注册三级技能：软缩放（比例重加权）、下限提升（区域级保证）、硬覆盖（阈值触发强制动作），按固定组合顺序协作。同时通过极简提示通道补充类别级语义线索，形成地图空间记忆+提示语义记忆的双重表征设计。

Result: 在MP3D、HM3D v0.1、HM3D v0.2三个基准上，SPL分别达到25.5、39.3、43.2，比最强先前方法绝对提升最高达6.0；在HM3D v0.1和v0.2上成功率分别达到69.7和75.9。

Conclusion: SkillNav通过将能力改进转化为技能注册（无需重训练VLM或干扰已有技能），为持续优化开辟了路径，且训练免费，实现了零样本目标导航的新最优结果。

Abstract: Vision-Language Model (VLM) agents have advanced zero-shot object-goal navigation, yet single-frame reasoning leaves them without the cross-step behavioral awareness an embodied navigator requires, producing recurring failures such as dead-end stalls, in-room loops, and circuitous approaches to detected targets. Prompt-based remedies inflate token budgets across multi-submodule episodes and still struggle to encode inherently spatial signals such as angles, map cells, and viewpoint coordinates. In this paper, we propose SkillNav, an extensible behavioral skill framework for VLM-based navigation that treats the curiosity value map already maintained by modern VLM navigators as a writable substrate on which composable skills inscribe behavioral memory at zero token cost. Skills are stratified into three tiers by their level of behavioral authority, namely soft scaling for proportional reweighting, lower-bound boost for region-level guarantees, and hard override for threshold-triggered forced actions, and cooperate across tiers under a fixed composition order that establishes a predictable, declared priority among skills. This design turns capability improvement into skill registration: new behaviors plug in without retraining the VLM or disturbing existing skills, opening a path for continual refinement. A minimal prompt channel complements the score-level skills with category-level semantic hints, yielding a dual-representation design in which spatial memory lives on the map and semantic memory in short prompts. Training-free, SkillNav establishes new state-of-the-art SPL across MP3D (25.5), HM3D v0.1 (39.3), and HM3D v0.2 (43.2), improving SPL by up to 6.0 absolute over the strongest prior method, and achieves the highest Success Rate on HM3D v0.1 (69.7) and v0.2 (75.9).

</details>


### [105] [Deployment-Ready UWB Localization for Industrial Ground Robots with Automatic Anchor Calibration and Terrain-Aware Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15807)
*Alexander Raab,Giulio Delama,Roland Jung,Stephan Weiss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端UWB定位管线，包含自动锚点标定和面向平面运动的多传感器融合估计器，在仓储AMR上实现了高精度定位，减少了人工标定工作量，并公开了数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB测距在工业AMR定位中具有高精度和低成本的优势，但实际部署面临两个主要挑战：静态锚点标定耗时且易出错，以及UWB与现有车载传感器的融合需要精心设计以确保鲁棒的位姿估计。本文旨在解决这些问题，降低人工干预，同时保持工业级精度。

Method: 提出端到端管线：(1) 自动锚点标定阶段，估计锚点位置和距离偏置；(2) 定位阶段，将UWB与本体感知融合到一种考虑偏置的扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）中，针对表面约束的车辆运动设计，无需大量参数调优。

Result: 在商业物流AMR的仓储环境中实现了准确的室内定位和室内外交界过渡定位，相比之前估计器公式一致性更好。在独立叉车数据集上的评估表明可迁移到其他平台。在有限视距和稀疏锚点覆盖的测试案例中仍然有效。

Conclusion: UWB定位可以大幅减少人工标定工作量的同时保持工业AMR所需的精度，证明了方法的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Ultra-Wideband (UWB) ranging has become a viable option for industrial Autonomous Mobile Robot (AMR) localization due to improved accuracy and low cost. However, real-world deployments remain limited by two recurring challenges: calibrating static anchors can be time-consuming and error-prone, and integrating UWB with existing onboard sensors requires careful design to ensure robust and consistent pose estimation. Addressing these challenges, this paper presents an end-to-end pipeline that combines automatic anchor calibration with a generic multi-sensor estimator tailored to surface-bound vehicle motion.
