It targets existing AMR stacks in scenarios where robot pose priors are available for initialization. The calibration stage estimates anchor positions and range biases, while the localization stage fuses UWB with proprioceptive sensing in a bias-aware Extended Kalman Filter to improve consistency without extensive parameter tuning. Experiments on a commercial logistics AMR in a warehouse setting demonstrate accurate positioning indoors and across outdoor transitions, with improved consistency compared to an earlier estimator formulation. Evaluation on an independent forklift dataset further indicates transferability to other platforms. The method remains effective in test cases with limited line-of-sight and sparse anchor coverage. These results show that UWB localization can be deployed with substantially reduced manual effort while preserving the accuracy required for industrial AMRs. The collected warehouse dataset is made publicly available.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Frontiers: Scene-Anomaly Guided Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15828)
*Akash Kumbar,Abhinav Raundhal,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAGE将自主3D探索重新定义为几何异常最小化问题，通过让机器人学习标准室内建筑先验知识，在导航中实时识别与先验不一致的场景异常（如破碎的墙壁、不完整的桌子），并主动前往解决这些异常的最佳视角，从而在实现高覆盖率的同時提升3D重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于覆盖最大化的几何启发式探索方法忽略了环境的结构上下文信息，导致探索轨迹效率低下并限制了最终3D重建的质量。本文旨在弥合空间覆盖与重建质量之间的差距。

Method: 提出SCAGE框架，将探索问题转化为几何异常最小化问题。机器人学习标准室内建筑的结构先验，在导航过程中持续将实时3D点云观测与学习到的先验进行对比，检测场景异常（几何不一致区域），并以这些异常作为引导信号驱动探索，主动前往最佳视角解决结构异常。

Result: 在多个场景中实现约90%的体积覆盖率，在3D重建质量上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将空间发现与高质量建图无缝耦合，SCAGE有效解决了传统探索方法忽视结构上下文的问题，证明了基于场景异常引导的探索范式在自主3D探索中的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration of unknown 3D environments is traditionally driven by coverage-maximizing geometric heuristics. However, these methods typically determine exploration targets without considering the underlying structural context. This leads to inefficient trajectories often limiting the fidelity of the final 3D reconstruction. To bridge the gap between spatial coverage and reconstruction quality, we introduce a novel paradigm: reframing exploration as a geometric anomaly minimization problem. We present SCAGE: SCene Anomaly Guided Exploration, a novel autonomous exploration framework that operates directly on unstructured 3D point clouds. Instead of blindly chasing volumetric boundaries, we equip the robot with a foundational understanding of standard indoor architecture. As the robot navigates, it continuously evaluates its live 3D observations against these learned expectations. When the incoming geometry contradicts the learned priors of a typical indoor environment, such as a fragmented wall or a partial table, the system flags these regions as scene anomalies. These geometric inconsistencies act as a guiding signal, naturally drawing the robot to investigate and resolve these structural anomalies from optimal vantage points. By actively targeting poorly reconstructed regions rather than just empty space, our approach seamlessly couples spatial discovery with high-fidelity mapping. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SCAGE achieves superior volumetric coverage (~90% in all scenes) and higher 3D reconstruction quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [107] [Minimum Time Dubins Airplane Paths with Asymmetric Climb Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15863)
*Jaeyoung Lim,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称Dubins飞机路径，考虑爬升和下降时不同的爬升率约束，相较于对称爬升率模型可减少71%的最小时间，并在采样规划任务中提速2.8倍，通过实际飞行验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Dubins飞机路径采用对称爬升率约束，导致路径次优且保守，无法充分利用飞行器的全部性能。

Method: 提出非对称Dubins飞机路径模型，在时间最优性条件下引入非对称爬升率约束，重新推导最优性条件。

Result: 随机状态连接最小时间减少71%；采样规划任务中位求解时间提升2.8倍；通过实际飞行验证可行性。

Conclusion: 非对称爬升率约束能更充分地利用飞行器性能，提升路径规划效率，并在实际场景中可行。

Abstract: Dubins airplane paths approximate the limited maneuverability of fixed-wing vehicles with minimum curvature and climb rate constraints. However, the symmetric climb rate constraints result in sub-optimal paths and conservative vehicle performance. In this work, we propose asymmetric Dubins airplane paths, which consider asymmetric climb rates for climbing and descending. We revisit the time optimality conditions and show that the asymmetric flight path angle constraints preserve optimality. We show that by considering asymmetric climb rates, we can take advantage of full performance of the vehicle, reducing the minimum time by 71% for connecting randomly generated states. We also demonstrate that the added climb rate results in 2.8 times faster to find the median solution time when integrated into a sampling-based planning task on rugged terrain, due to the added feasibility. We further demonstrate the practicality of the approach with a real-world flight.

</details>


### [108] [Dynamics-Aware Meta-Imitation for Generalization to Unseen Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15880)
*Zhenduo Shang,Xiyao Liu,Bohan Li,Xudong Wang,Teng Ren,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动力学感知元模仿学习框架（DAMI），通过元学习构建共享技能空间，结合VMT模块捕获时空动力学、U2T模块融合非结构多模态观测及TCFM机制调制3D特征，实现机器人从少量演示中快速泛化至未见任务，在仿真和真实实验中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法面临数据稀缺和环境泛化能力不足的问题，主要局限于领域内任务演示学习，难以泛化到未见任务。

Method: 1) 元学习构建共享技能空间实现快速适应；2) Visual-Motor Trajectory (VMT)模块捕获任务隐空间中的复杂时空动力学；3) Unpaired Unified Task (U2T)模块融合非结构多模态观测；4) Task-Conditioned Feature Modulation (TCFM)机制调制底层3D特征。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，DAMI在已见任务的直接推理和未见任务的少样本微调适应上均优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过捕获随机完整参考演示中的内在动力学，框架学习底层任务逻辑而非记忆静态线索，从而确保有效泛化。

Abstract: Imitation Learning aims to learn skills from extensive observations and demonstrations for robots, so it suffers from data scarcity and environment generalization. The existing methods predominantly focus on imitation from in-domain tasks and consequently struggle with generalization to unseen tasks. To bridge this generalization gap, we propose the \textbf{D}ynamics-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{M}eta-\textbf{I}mitation (DAMI) framework. By integrating meta-learning to construct a shared skill space, DAMI equips agents for rapid adaptation to novel tasks. We introduce the Visual-Motor Trajectory (VMT) module to capture complex spatio-temporal dynamics within the task latent space. Furthermore, we propose the Unpaired Unified Task (U2T) block to fuse unstructured multimodal observations. To coordinate these representations, we integrate a Task-Conditioned Feature Modulation (TCFM) mechanism customized for modulating low-level 3D features. By capturing intrinsic dynamics from a random complete reference demonstration, our framework learns the underlying task logic rather than memorizing static cues, ensuring effective generalization. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines regarding direct inference on seen tasks and adaptation to unseen tasks via few-shot fine-tuning.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Reach-Avoid Task with Reinforcement Learning: Vectorized Simulation and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15935)
*Jonas Weihing,Shahram Eivazi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一个无需简化的、真实复杂的机器人手臂触及-避让任务基准测试环境，利用MuJoCo MJX和Brax库实现了并行化模拟与DRL训练，在UR5e和Franka机器人上取得了先进的成功率，同时揭示DRL在简化场景中表现完美，但在真实场景中性能显著下降，表明该任务仍需进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL在触及-避让任务中的研究多局限于简化的桌面环境，无法反映真实世界的复杂性。本文旨在构建一个无简化的、真实的基准测试环境，以评估DRL在复杂场景下的实际能力。

Method: 利用MuJoCo MJX物理引擎和Brax库实现仿真环境与DRL算法的并行化加速，构建了涵盖多样化设置的机器人手臂触及-避让任务基准测试，并在UR5e和Franka Emika Robot上进行了评估。

Result: 在触及任务中，UR5e成功率达96.1%，Franka达98.8%；在静态触及-避让任务中，UR5e成功率达86.8%，Franka达95.2%。实验表明DRL在简化场景中表现完美，但在真实复杂场景下性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 尽管DRL在简化环境下能完美解决触及任务，但在真实复杂场景中性能大幅下降，声明完全解决机器人手臂触及-避让任务仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has a longstanding tradition in addressing the reach-avoid task problem, especially for controlling robotic arms. While this task serves as a baseline environment within the research community, the ability of DRL to effectively learn the each-avoid task in complex and realistic scenarios beyond simplified and restricted tabletop settings remains uncertain. In this paper, we present, for the first time, a comprehensive benchmark for the reachavoid task that accurately captures real-world complexities without simplifications. We demonstrate a diverse range of settings for robotic arm reach-avoid task, which can be used for evaluating DRL research. We achieved this by utilizing the MuJoCo MJX physics engine and parallelizing both the simulation environment and DRL algorithms using the Brax library. We achieved state-of-the-art results with success rates of 96.1% (UR5e) and 98.8% (Franka Emika Robot) for the reach task and 86.8% (UR5e) and 95.2% (Franka) for the static reachavoid task. Our results indicate that while in previous works DRL agents could solve, for example, a reach task in a simplified setting perfectly, their agents performance collapses when evaluated in realistic scenarios. Overall, this work identifies that additional research is still required to claim the successful resolution of the robotic arm reach-avoid task using DRL. The environment and benchmarking code is available as open source at the following link

</details>


### [110] [Embodied Active Learning under Limited Annotation and Navigation Budget for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15974)
*Hadrien Crassous,Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Minahil Raza,Joni Pajarinen,Riad Akrour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在机器人导航时间和注释预算双重约束下，通过空间不一致性引导的具身主动学习方法，用于自适应调整目标检测器到未知环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉目标检测器在部署到未知环境时性能下降，而传统主动学习未考虑机器人导航的物理约束（移动成本）和注释预算限制。

Method: 提出具身批量主动学习框架，每轮中机器人在有限导航预算下收集候选样本，并在有限注释预算下选择最相关的图像。利用空间一致性识别标签不一致的图像，针对性处理检测器的失败案例，无需外部监督。

Result: 在AI2-THOR模拟器和Boston Dynamics Spot机器人（搭载YOLOv5）上的实验表明，该方法在相同预算下实现了最高的检测精度，优于多个基线方法。

Conclusion: 空间不一致性能够有效指导机器人导航和图像选择，在预算约束下实现自适应目标检测，且该方法已开源。

Abstract: This paper studies how to adapt a computer vision object detector to an unknown environment under both a robot navigation time and annotation budget constraint. Our approach selects informative robot trajectories and image samples to retrain the detector, explicitly targeting its failure cases. Formally, the approach is an embodied variant of batch active learning, where at each round an agent has a limited navigation budget to collect candidate samples and a limited annotation budget for the most relevant images. We leverage spatial consistency to identify images with inconsistent labels, which are likely to provide the greatest improvement to the vision model. We evaluate the approach using different active learning objectives on large scenes from the AI2-THOR simulator and on a real-world setup using a Boston Dynamics Spot robot with the real-time object detector YOLOv5. Through comparison against several baselines, our experimental results show that spatial inconsistency helps guide the agent and select relevant images without external supervision, achieving the highest detection accuracy at the end of the adaptation process under the same budget. The open-source project can be found at https://mkabouri.github.io/embodied-active-learning-od

</details>


### [111] [Data and Learning Where it Matters for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15982)
*Oliver Hausdörfer,Linus Schwarz,Gabor Marko,Christian Dietz,Timo Class,Luka Hofer,Jim Yun-Jin Li,Johannes Hechtl,Ralf Römer,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化数据收集方案，结合离线深度强化学习，仅对接触密集型任务的关键片段进行密集数据收集，其余自由空间运动使用传统规划方法。在四个真实世界任务中，仅用2-2.5小时自主数据收集，平均成功率96%（基线55%），且在分布外场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习策略虽然在大型数据集上表现良好，但在高精度任务中仍然脆弱且泛化能力差。作者发现这些局限性主要源于数据收集缺乏结构性和针对性。

Method: 提出针对接触密集型任务关键片段自动化数据收集的方案，结合离线深度强化学习，摒弃对遥操作技能和在线策略更新的依赖；非关键的自由空间运动则使用传统规划方法处理。

Result: 在四个真实世界接触密集型任务中，仅需2-2.5小时自主数据收集，平均成功率96%，远超最强基线55%；在分布外场景中性能依然保持高水平。

Conclusion: 该工作为接触密集型任务的针对性数据收集铺平了道路，实现了高精度应用中的高成功率，证明了结构化的、关键片段导向的数据收集策略的有效性。

Abstract: Learned policies trained end-to-end on large datasets often remain brittle in high-precision tasks and struggle with generalization. We find that these limitations largely stem from a lack of structure and focus in data collection. Our key insight is to leverage dense data collection only for the critical segment of contact-rich tasks and to rely on traditional planning during simple free-space motion. We propose an automated data-collection scheme in combination with offline deep reinforcement learning for the critical segment of the task, eliminating reliance on a teleoperator's skill and on online policy updates. Across four challenging real-world tasks, using only 2 to 2.5 hours of autonomous data collection, we achieve an average success rate of 96%, compared to the strongest baseline at 55%. Notably, performance remains high in out-of-distribution scenarios where end-to-end approaches struggle. Our results pave the way for targeted data collection for contact-rich tasks and for high success rates in precision applications.

</details>


### [112] [A Morphing-Designed Hexarotor Prototype combining Practical Resilience and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16002)
*Murat Bronz,Mahmoud Hamandi,Elgiz Baskaya,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明了一种名为Opti-Hexa的六旋翼原型机，该原型机在单螺旋桨故障时具有实际鲁棒性，同时在与标准星形原型机相同尺寸、重量、硬件和软件条件下，能效相当。利用新型开源变形平台，通过改变相邻螺旋桨之间的角度，研究了连续几何范围内的权衡。通过数据拟合的经验功率模型研究实际效率，并通过比较故障时的位置精度和旋转动能与名义悬停条件下的观测值来评估实际鲁棒性。实验证实了该特定变形平台的几何可行区域，在该区域内，既保证了鲁棒性，又避免了现有技术中实际鲁棒设计通常伴随的空气动力学效率损失。平台硬件和软件已完全开源以支持进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机设计在鲁棒性（如单螺旋桨故障容错）与能效之间存在权衡，通常鲁棒设计会牺牲空气动力学效率。本文旨在探索是否存在一种几何构型，能够在保持能效的同时实现实际鲁棒性。

Method: 基于开源变形平台，通过连续改变相邻螺旋桨角度来探索几何空间；利用数据拟合的经验功率模型量化实际效率；通过比较故障时的位置精度和旋转动能与悬停状态下的指标来评估实际鲁棒性；进行实验验证。

Result: 证实了Opti-Hexa特定变形平台存在几何可行区域，在该区域内单螺旋桨故障鲁棒性与能效可以兼得，避免了传统鲁棒设计的空气动力学效率损失。

Conclusion: Opti-Hexa通过几何优化打破了鲁棒性与能效之间的传统权衡，为容错多旋翼设计提供了新范式，并且开源平台有助于后续研究。

Abstract: This work demonstrates experimentally the existence of a hexarotor prototype, termed Opti-Hexa, that simultaneously achieves practical resilience to single-propeller failures and energy efficiency comparable to a standard Star-shaped prototype with the same size, weight, hardware and software. Leveraging a novel open-source morphing platform, we investigate the trade-offs across a continuous range of geometries by varying the angles between adjacent propellers. We study practical efficiency through a data-fitted empirical power model and evaluate practical resilience by comparing the position accuracy and rotational kinetic energy during failure to those observed under nominal hovering conditions. Our experiments confirm the existence of a geometric viability region for this specific morphing platform, where resilience is ensured without the aerodynamic efficiency losses typically associated with practically resilient designs found in the state of the art. The complete hardware and software of the morphing platform are released to support further research.

</details>


### [113] [Let the Body Follow: Coupled Egocentric Control for Whole-Body Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16095)
*Tsung-Chi Lin,Yichen Xie,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了耦合自我中心控制（coupled egocentric control）方法，通过将机器人躯干和底盘的移动与操作者的头部和手臂运动自动耦合，实现了全身遥操作，在TIAGo移动机械手上验证了该方法相比传统混合界面在操作效率、认知负荷和用户偏好等方面的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身遥操作需要用户同时协调机器人头部、手臂、躯干和底盘等多个组件的感知、操控、姿态和移动，这种多维度协调对于用户来说难度极大，尤其是在保持任务感知的同时避免运动学和环境约束。现有方法通常需要用户通过触控板等显式指令控制每个组件，增加了操作负担。

Method: 提出耦合自我中心控制方法：头部俯仰控制躯干高度，头部偏航驱动底盘旋转，末端执行器高度控制躯干运动，末端执行器工作空间边界触发底盘平移。用户只需专注于视线和手部控制，躯干和底盘自动响应。

Result: 在面向家庭护理任务的TIAGo移动机械手全身遥操作用户研究中，相比基线混合接口，耦合自我中心控制显著提升了物体操作效率，减少了基于按钮的控制操作和手臂奇异位形，降低了心理需求和整体工作负荷，提高了易用性、易学性、信心以及用户在躯干和底盘控制方面的偏好。

Conclusion: 耦合自我中心控制作为一种身体跟随式遥操作方法，通过自动化躯干和底盘的响应，有效降低了全身遥操作的多维度协调难度，提升了操作效率和用户体验，为移动机械手的直观遥操作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Whole-body teleoperation requires users to coordinate perception, manipulation, posture, and mobility across multiple robot components. This coordination is difficult because users must simultaneously control the robot's head, arms, torso, and base while maintaining task awareness and avoiding kinematic or environmental constraints. In this paper, we propose coupled egocentric control, a body-following teleoperation approach in which the robot's torso and base automatically respond to the operator's head and arm motions. Rather than requiring explicit touchpad commands for every torso or base adjustment, the system lets users focus on gaze and hand control: head pitch adjusts torso height, head yaw drives base rotation, end-effector height adjusts torso motion, and end-effector workspace boundaries trigger base translation. We evaluate this approach in a user study on whole-body teleoperation of a TIAGo mobile manipulator for home-care-inspired tasks. Compared with a baseline hybrid interface, coupled egocentric control improves object manipulation efficiency, reduces button-based control effort and arm singularities, lowers mental demand and overall workload, and increases ease of use, ease of learning, confidence, and user preference for torso and base control.

</details>


### [114] [BayesContact: Uncertain Pose Estimation via Visuo-Tactile Proposals and Simulation-based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16123)
*Aditya Kamireddypalli,Matias Mattamala,Joao Moura,Russell Buchanan,Sethu Vijayakumar,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BayesContact，一种基于仿真的推理框架，通过融合深度视觉和力/触觉信息进行位姿估计，在插销入孔任务中相比纯视觉方法将位姿可观测性和插入成功率提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要高精度位姿估计，纯深度感知难以满足；现有依赖视觉和接触的方法需要昂贵的离线训练且难以泛化到新环境和新几何形状。

Method: 使用粒子滤波维护物体位姿信念；通过渲染器预测深度观测似然，通过物理仿真器预测接触观测似然；结合真实观测更新后验信念；利用多模态信念的信息增益主动选择探测动作进行歧义消解。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，相比纯视觉推理，位姿可观测性提升30%，插入成功率提升30%。

Conclusion: 基于仿真的推理框架有效融合视觉和触觉信息，无需离线训练即可泛化到新环境和新几何形状，显著提升接触丰富任务的位姿估计性能。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires pose estimates that are often more accurate than what depth-only sensing provides. Existing methods, relying on vision and contact, employ costly offline training procedures that need to be retrained for new environments and geometries. We propose BayesContact, a Simulation-Based Inference framework for visuo-tactile pose estimation in peg-in-hole insertion. BayesContact maintains a particle belief over object pose and fuses depth observations with force/torque-derived contact evidence. We employ simulation based forward models to approximate these observation likelihoods. For each pose hypothesis, a renderer predicts depth measurements and a physics simulator predicts contact outcomes under guarded probing actions; both are scored against real observations to update the belief. The resulting multimodal belief also enables information-gain-based probing for active disambiguation. Across simulated geometries and real-robot experiments, BayesContact improves pose observability and insertion success over vision-only inference by 30%

</details>


### [115] [A New Implementation of NeoSLAM and a Comparative Evaluation with RatSLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16143)
*Joao Victor T. Borges,Fabio Coelho,Paulo Padrao,Jose Fuentes,Ramon R. Costa,Liu Hsu,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于ROS2模块化架构重写的NeoSLAM算法，实现了实时执行并最小化数据丢弃，在三个数据集上与RatSLAM进行了比较评估，结果显示新实现在处理吞吐量上优于原版，地图重建性能与RatSLAM相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NeoSLAM实现在实时执行方面存在局限，需要一种模块化、现代化的实现以提升处理吞吐量并减少输入数据丢弃，从而增强实际应用中的实用性。

Method: 将NeoSLAM算法完全重写为基于ROS2的模块化架构，利用现代框架实现实时执行；并在三个不同环境条件的数据集上与RatSLAM进行对比评估。

Result: 新NeoSLAM实现在处理吞吐量上优于原版，满足实时应用需求；地图重建性能与RatSLAM相当；在映射一致性和轨迹重建方面展示了有效性。

Conclusion: 基于ROS2模块化重写的NeoSLAM在实时性能和实际应用可行性上具有显著优势，同时保持了与RatSLAM可比的地图重建质量。

Abstract: This paper presents a new implementation of the NeoSLAM algorithm. The proposed version is a complete rewrite of NeoSLAM into a modular architecture using modern frameworks that, together, enable real-time execution with minimal discarding of input data. This work also provides a comparative evaluation between NeoSLAM and RatSLAM across three datasets under varying environmental conditions. The experimental results highlight differences in mapping consistency and trajectory reconstruction, demonstrating the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed ROS2-based implementation. The results indicate that the new NeoSLAM outperforms the original in terms of processing throughput for real-time applications and achieves comparable performance to RatSLAM in terms of map reconstruction across the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [116] [VTLoc: Learning-based Tactile Contact Localization in Visual Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16146)
*Zhiyuan Wu,Zhuo Chen,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VTLoc是一个视觉-触觉融合框架，通过几何多模态对齐模块和迭代定位更新器，利用3D点云作为视觉输入，实现触觉接触点在物体表面的精确定位，在100个真实物体的基准测试中显著提升了单点触觉接触定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉提供全局物体上下文，触觉提供接触点的精确局部信息，但两者之间的空间对齐存在挑战，难以准确预测触觉在物体表面的接触位置。

Method: 提出VTLoc框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 几何多模态对齐模块——从融合的视觉-触觉特征重建伪点云，并与视觉点云对齐以强制执行跨模态空间一致性；(2) 迭代定位更新器——利用融合特征迭代优化预测的接触位置。

Result: 在100个真实物体的新基准测试上，VTLoc通过减少局部到全局的对应歧义，提升了单点触觉接触定位性能。

Conclusion: 视觉-触觉融合结合几何对齐与迭代优化策略，能有效解决接触定位中的跨模态空间对齐问题，提升定位精度。

Abstract: Vision and touch are complementary modalities essential for robotic perception and manipulation. While vision provides global object context, touch offers precise local information at contact points. Integrating these modalities for contact localization, i.e., predicting the location of touch on an object's surface, poses significant challenges due to the need for accurate spatial alignment between tactile data and visual geometry. To address this challenge, we propose VTLoc, a novel visual-tactile framework that localizes contact points from tactile readings using a 3D point cloud as visual input. VTLoc introduces two key components: a geometric multi-modal alignment module, which reconstructs a pseudo-point cloud from fused visual-tactile features and aligns it with the visual point cloud to enforce spatial consistencies across modalities; and an iterative localizing updater, which iteratively refines the predicted contact location using fused visual-tactile features. Evaluated on a new benchmark of 100 real-world objects, VTLoc improves single-touch contact localization by reducing local-to-global correspondence ambiguity.

</details>


### [117] [Vision-Language-Motion Maps: An Open-Vocabulary, Uncertainty-Aware, Queryable Motion Attribute for 3D Scene Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16173)
*Dibyendu Ghosh,Ayushi Shakya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLMM（视觉-语言-运动地图），一种开放词汇、可自然语言查询的3D地图表示，每个场景元素融合了语义运动先验和几何观测运动，并带有逐元素不确定性，支持区分已动、可动未动和静止的物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D地图只能回答静态场景中"什么在哪"的查询，无法回答场景元素如何运动的问题（如"什么在移动""什么可以移动"），缺乏对运动属性的建模能力。

Method: VLMM为每个地图元素融合两个运动属性：VLM/LLM提供的语义可动性先验（推断物体能否移动）和几何上观测到的跨帧运动（实际是否移动），并附加逐元素不确定性度量。查询被转化为属性过滤器。

Result: 在AI2-THOR模拟器上消融实验表明语义与几何运动字段不可互相替代；在真实TUM/Bonn数据集上，不确定性通道持续提升动/静分类AP，降低误报，且对噪声位姿鲁棒；后验等渗校准后ECE达0.10。

Conclusion: VLMM是首个同时具备开放词汇、自然语言可查询、融合先验与观测运动、逐元素不确定性四个特性的3D地图表示，填补了现有工作的空白。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D maps let robots answer language queries about what and where, but they assume a static world and cannot answer queries about how scene elements behave. We introduce Vision-Language-Motion Maps (VLMM), an open-vocabulary, natural-language-queryable 3D map in which each element carries a fused motion attribute: a VLM/LLM semantic movability prior combined with geometrically observed cross-frame motion, together with a per-element uncertainty. Queries reduce to attribute filters that distinguish what has been seen to move, what could move but has not, and what stays still. On a controlled simulator benchmark with exact ground truth (AI2-THOR, three scene types) we show through ablation that the schema fields are non-substitutable: a semantic-only baseline fails motion queries even with strong features, and neither motion field substitutes for the other (the prior cannot answer "what is moving," observed motion cannot answer "what could move"). On real dynamic RGB-D (TUM and Bonn, six sequences) we show the uncertainty channel-our key difference from prior fused-motion work-consistently improves moving-vs-static average precision and reduces false motion flags, and is robust to estimated (noisy) poses. The raw confidence is not calibrated, but post-hoc isotonic calibration reaches an expected calibration error of 0.10. VLMM is a representation contribution: the closest prior maps each lack at least one of the four properties-open-vocabulary, language-queryable, fused prior-and-observed motion, and per-element uncertainty-that our combination provides.

</details>


### [118] [Handroid: Bridging Dexterous Hand and Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16187)
*Ruogu Li,Chenyang Ma,Sikai Li,Zhenyu Wei,Yunchao Yao,Haochen Shi,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Handroid是一种桌面级双形态机器人平台，通过重新配置同一套27自由度机电系统，可在灵巧手（20自由度拟人结构）和桌面人形（含12自由度下肢的完整人形）两种形态间切换，支持灵巧操作、人形运动以及跨形态学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上灵巧手和人形机器人被开发为独立的实体形态，前者擅长物体尺度的接触丰富操作，后者提供以人为中心环境中的移动和全身交互。作者希望将两种能力集成到单一可重构平台上，以紧凑、可复现的方式推进形态可重构机器人和跨形态机器人学习。

Method: 设计了一个27自由度的机电身体，可在两种形态间重用：灵巧手形态下20个自由度构成与人类手部运动学结构高度匹配的拟人手；人形形态下将相同关节模块重新配置为带头部、手臂和腿部的桌面人形（12自由度下肢用于运动和全身动作）。同时提供统一控制和学习框架，支持手部遥操作、灵巧抓取、手中操作、人形运动、步态生成和交互式动作编排。

Result: 通过真实世界灵巧操作、基于强化学习的运动控制、关键帧动作部署以及涉及形态重配置、移动、对接和灵巧抓取的长时序任务进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: Handroid作为紧凑且可复现的平台，为形态可重构机器人和跨形态机器人学习提供了有效的硬件和软件基础。

Abstract: Dexterous hands and humanoid robots are typically developed as distinct embodiments: the former enable contact-rich manipulation at the object scale, whereas the latter provide mobility and whole-body interaction in human-centered environments. We introduce \textbf{Handroid}, a desktop-scale dual-embodiment robot that integrates both capabilities within a single reconfigurable platform. Handroid reuses one 27-DoF electromechanical body as either a dexterous hand or a desktop humanoid, measuring 0.33 m in height and 2.05 kg in weight. In the dexterous hand embodiment, 20 DoFs form an anthropomorphic hand closely matching the kinematic structure of the human hand. In the humanoid embodiment, the same articulated modules are reconfigured into a humanoid with a head, arms, and legs, including a 12-DoF lower-limb structure for locomotion and whole-body motion. Handroid further provides a unified control and learning framework supporting hand teleoperation, dexterous grasping, in-hand manipulation, humanoid locomotion, gait generation, and interactive motion authoring. We validate the platform through real-world dexterous manipulation, reinforcement-learning-based locomotion, keyframe motion deployment, and a long-horizon task involving embodiment reconfiguration, locomotion, docking, and dexterous pick-and-place. These results position Handroid as a compact and reproducible platform for advancing morphology-reconfigurable robotics and cross-embodiment robot learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [GraphDx: A Cost-Aware Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Agent Framework for Sequential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15280)
*Shaoting Tan,Ning Liu,Yuntao Du,Shuyue Wei,Wu Shuai,Qian Li,Yanyu Xu,Wei Zhang,Lizhen Cui,Haitao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphDx 提出了一种基于知识增强的框架，通过构建带有量化典型性、以动作为中心的拓扑结构和双目标属性的医学诊断知识图谱（MDKG），并结合三个协作代理（感知、推理和决策），在多个LLM骨干上实现了诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，同时测试成本降低20-54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法存在知识-推理鸿沟：尽管编码了广泛的医学知识，LLM在成本约束下难以进行系统性推理，常导致过度测试。

Method: 提出GraphDx框架，包含两个核心创新：(1) 自动化流水线构建MDKG，具有量化典型性、动作中心拓扑和双目标属性（诊断相关性和成本敏感性）；(2) 三个协作代理——感知代理和决策代理负责语言理解和生成，推理代理在MDKG上进行确定性证据评分和成本感知规划。

Result: 在MedQA和MIMIC-IV数据集上，使用DeepSeek-V3、Kimi-k2、Llama-3.3三个LLM骨干，诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，测试成本降低20-54%。

Conclusion: GraphDx为自动化临床诊断提供了稳健、经济且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential diagnosis requires balancing diagnostic accuracy against resource costs through iterative information gathering. Existing Large Language Model (LLM) approaches exhibit a critical knowledge-reasoning gap: despite encoding extensive medical knowledge, they struggle to reason systematically under cost constraints, often resorting to excessive testing. We propose GraphDx, a knowledge-enhanced framework with two core innovations. First, we design an automated pipeline that leverages LLMs to construct Medical Diagnosis Knowledge Graphs (MDKGs) with quantized typicality, action-centric topology, and dual-objective attributes for both diagnostic relevance and cost-sensitivity. Second, we introduce three collaborative agents (Perception, Reasoning, and Decision) where the Perception and Decision Agents handle language understanding and generation, while the Reasoning Agent performs deterministic evidence scoring and cost-aware planning on the MDKG. Experiments on MedQA and MIMIC-IV across three LLM backbones (DeepSeek-V3, Kimi-k2, Llama-3.3) show that GraphDx improves diagnostic success rates from 50--68% to 79--93% while reducing test costs by 20--54%, providing a robust, economical, and interpretable solution for automated clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [120] [Causal-Audit: Explicit and Auditable Graph-based Reasoning via Target-Aware Causal Chain Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15281)
*Su Lan,Xuefei Yin,Yanming Zhu,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种显式且可审计的因果推理框架，用于无上下文干预问答。该方法通过构建目标感知因果图、路径级因果证据聚合等模块化阶段，替代隐式端到端推理，在三个基准上优于现有LLM方法，并提供可解释的因果推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法依赖隐式语言层面推理，导致因果假设不透明、推理路径不可验证，尤其在无上下文设置下对复杂干预的预测脆弱。需要一种显式、可审计的因果推理机制推动LLM超越表面相关性，理解底层因果机制。

Method: 提出四个模块化阶段的因果推理框架：(1) 目标感知因果图构建策略，将目标变量作为核心约束进行图扩展，抑制无关变量和虚假因果；(2) 路径级因果证据聚合机制，组合多条因果路径并建模增强与抵消效应，实现超越单链推理的鲁棒决策。

Result: 在三个基准上的实验表明，该框架持续优于现有基于LLM的方法，同时提供可解释和可审计的因果推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 显式结构化因果推理能够有效提升LLM在无上下文干预问答任务中的表现，透明且可审计的推理过程是该范式的关键优势。

Abstract: Causal and intervention-based question answering is fundamental to advancing large language models (LLMs) toward reasoning beyond surface-level correlations and understanding underlying causal mechanisms. However, existing LLM-based methods often rely on implicit language-level reasoning, resulting in opaque causal assumptions, unverifiable reasoning paths, and fragile predictions under complex interventions, particularly in context-free settings. In this paper, we propose an explicit and auditable causal reasoning framework for context-free intervention-based question answering. Our method formulates causal inference as structured reasoning over an explicit causal graph through four modular stages, rather than implicit end-to-end prediction. A key innovation is a target-aware causal graph construction strategy that treats the target variable as a core constraint during graph expansion, effectively suppressing irrelevant variables, spurious causal relations, and reasoning noise. We further introduce a path-level causal evidence aggregation mechanism that combines multiple causal paths while modeling both reinforcing and counteracting effects, enabling robust decision-making beyond single-chain reasoning. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing LLM-based methods while providing interpretable and auditable causal reasoning traces.

</details>


### [121] [Cura 1T: Specialized Model for Agentic Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15314)
*actAVA AI,:,Haolin Chen,Leon Qi,Steve Brown,Deon Metelski,Tao Xia,Joonyul Lee,Qixuan Wang,Kevin Riley,Frank Wang,Weiran Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cura 1T，一个通过人类门控自进化循环训练的医疗专用大语言模型，能同时处理患者咨询、临床推理、交互诊断和电子病历工具使用等任务，在医疗评估中达到前沿水平且不牺牲通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗大语言模型在覆盖高风险的医患沟通、专家级临床推理、工作流执行等多场景能力上存在局限，且针对单一任务的改进往往会降低其他任务的表现，缺乏系统性进化机制。

Method: Cura 1T采用人类门控自进化循环训练框架。每个进化轮次中：训练代理规划目标能力→训练模型→评估基准轨迹→根据观测到的失败模式精炼数据混合策略。该数据中心的循环通过针对性合成数据和精选示例持续改进模型，而非单次通用的医疗数据更新。

Result: 在医疗评估套件中，Cura 1T在所有前沿基线模型中排名前列或最高，同时在领域外推理和智能体基准测试上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 人类门控的自进化循环是一种有效的医疗LLM训练范式，能够在多任务场景下实现定向能力提升而避免灾难性遗忘，为构建综合性医疗AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Healthcare spans high-stakes communication, expert reasoning, and workflow execution, yet specialized LLMs that cover these use cases together remain limited. A healthcare model must handle patient consultation, clinical reasoning over text and images, interactive diagnosis, and electronic health record (EHR) tool use. These capabilities fail in different ways, and a narrow update for one task can degrade another. We present Cura 1T, a healthcare-specialized LLM trained through a human-gated self-evolution loop. In each evolution round, a training agent plans a target capability, trains the model, evaluates benchmark trajectories, and refines the data mixture from observed failures. This data-centered loop improves the model through targeted synthetic and curated examples rather than a single generic medical-data update. Across the healthcare evaluation suite, Cura 1T ranks at or near the top among frontier baselines, while remaining competitive on out-of-domain reasoning and agentic benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [AnovaX: A Local, Multi-Agent Voice Assistant with LLM Planning, Typed Executors, and Adaptive Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15367)
*Raunak B Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnovaX 是一个完全在本地运行、以桌面为操作表面的语音助手，通过单进程 Python 串联唤醒词、语音识别、LLM 规划器、安全层、多智能体编排器和自适应恢复循环，支持手机远程控制，仅需数千行代码即可完成应用操控、搜索、并发任务协调和故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前桌面语音助手仍依赖云端管道传输原始音频且功能固定，缺乏本地化、可扩展的桌面操作能力。作者希望证明一个轻量级本地助手足以替代传统云端方案完成复杂的桌面交互任务。

Method: 单进程 Python 系统，集成唤醒词门控、语音处理管道、Gemini LLM 规划器输出 JSON 工具调用计划、白名单/黑名单安全层、多智能体编排器（每种工具对应专用智能体类，含超时/重试/资源锁）、递归 MetaAgent（最多两层嵌套）、基于 ReAct 提示的自适应恢复循环（对只读工具投机执行以隐藏延迟），以及 Flask 服务器提供本地 WiFi 手机远程控制和 MJPEG 屏幕流。

Result: 系统能以数千行代码实现打开应用、输入文本、运行搜索、协调并发动作、从单步失败中恢复，并完全通过手机远程驱动，无需 LLM 直接接触键盘。

Conclusion: 一个可理解、轻量级的本地优先助手足以胜任复杂的桌面交互任务，无需依赖云端管道或固定技能集，展示了本地 LLM 助手的可行性和灵活性。

Abstract: Desktop voice assistants are still dominated by cloud pipelines that ship raw audio off the machine and expose a fixed set of skills. We describe AnovaX, a small local-first assistant that runs entirely on the user's computer and treats the desktop itself as its action surface. A single Python process wires together a wake-word gate, a speech pipeline, an LLM planner (Gemini) that emits a JSON plan of tool calls, a whitelist-and-denylist safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator that translates each plan into typed child agents on a bounded thread pool, and an adaptive recovery loop that takes over whenever a core step fails. Every tool corresponds to a specialized agent class (AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent and six others) with its own timeout, retry policy, and shared-resource locks. A recursive MetaAgent lets the planner delegate a sub-goal back to itself, capped at two levels of nesting. The recovery loop uses a compact ReAct-style prompt and hides Gemini's latency behind speculative execution of read-only tools. A companion Flask server exposes a phone-friendly remote over the local WiFi, mirrors every agent lifecycle event to the phone in real time, and streams the laptop's screen back over MJPEG so the user can watch remote commands land as they run. The point of the project is less to compete with Siri or Alexa than to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant is enough to open apps, type into them, run searches, coordinate concurrent actions, recover from single-step failures, and be driven entirely from a phone in another room -- without the LLM ever touching the keyboard.

</details>


### [123] [Precise but Uncoupled: Reviewer Precision Does Not Guarantee Critique Uptake in Multi-Agent Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15388)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Jingyan Jiang,Vikram Vasudevan,Cheng-Hau Yang,Huihuo Zheng,Le Chen,Eliu A. Huerta,Venkatram Vishwanath,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在数学推理智能体系统中，审查者角色虽然能准确发现错误，但批评意见若无法有效改变后续答案，则系统性能提升有限。广播式同行讨论优于规划-执行-审查流水线，因为后者批评采纳率低，即使审查精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证“专门的审查阶段应能将错误候选转化为正确候选”这一常见假设，探究审查者质量与批评采纳率之间的分离现象。

Method: 在4,181个Omni-MATH问题上，使用匹配的gpt-oss-120b模型，对比广播式同行讨论与规划-执行-审查流水线（PER）的性能，并通过控制实验分析审查精度、批评采纳率及不同干预方式的影响。

Result: 广播式在较难题集上优于PER；PER审查者精度更高（0.861 vs 0.644），但有用批评更少改变候选答案，且审查引导修复效果更差；强制显式确认降低准确率，嵌入审查上下文可部分改善但无法消除差距。

Conclusion: 审查者质量与批评行动效果可分离；以审查者为中心的评估可能高估系统质量——协议能发现错误但不一定能解决更多问题。

Abstract: Many math- and science-oriented agent systems use hierarchical designs with specialized reviewer roles, assuming that a dedicated review stage should help turn wrong candidates into correct ones. We test this assumption on 4,181 verifier-grounded Omni-MATH problems using matched gpt-oss-120b actors. Collaboration adds little on the easiest tiers, but from tier 4 onward the gains open sharply; in this harder regime, broadcast-style peer discussion reaches higher final accuracy than a planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER). We ask whether this gap is explained by reviewer quality or by whether critique changes the next answer the protocol carries forward. It is not explained by reviewer precision alone: PER's reviewer is more precise than broadcast's (0.861 vs. 0.644), yet evaluator-verified useful critique is much less likely to change the next candidate and produces lower reviewer-guided repair. These results show that reviewer detection quality and critique uptake are empirically separable. Within matched PER interventions, forcing explicit acknowledgment lowers final accuracy, while embedding reviewer guidance directly in the solver's working context partially improves follow-through without closing the gap. Overall, reviewer-centric evaluation can overstate system quality: a protocol may spot errors well yet still fail to solve more problems if it does not act on those critiques.

</details>


### [124] [DrawingVQA: A Real-World Benchmark for Multi-Depth Visual-Textual Reasoning on Construction Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15418)
*Yoonhwa Jung,Junryu Fu,Mani Golparvar-Fard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DrawingVQA，首个面向真实建筑施工图纸的多模态大模型评估基准，包含33张施工图与92个专家标注的问答对，覆盖感知理解、上下文解释和领域专家推理三个深度层次，并设计了双分类框架从7个工程维度和4个MLLM能力维度评估模型。实验表明当前最先进MLLM与专家性能之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦自然图像或简单平面图，缺乏对施工图纸这一融合抽象几何、符号标注、表格数据和专业文本的复杂视觉-文本领域的评估，无法反映MLLM在真实工程工作流中的能力。

Method: 构建33张"Issued for Construction"施工图纸和92个专家精心设计的问答对，定义三个推理深度（感知理解、上下文解释、领域专家推理），并提出双分类框架从7个建筑工程维度和4个MLLM能力维度进行联合分析。

Result: 当前最先进MLLM在高推理深度任务上表现显著低于专家水平，表明模型在面向工程工作流的专业多模态推理方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: DrawingVQA为专业领域多模态推理评估奠定了基础，有望推动AI理解与真实工程工作流的整合。

Abstract: We introduce DrawingVQA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on real-world construction drawings -- a core media in architecture, civil, and many other engineering practices. Unlike natural images or schematic floor plans, construction drawings fuse abstract geometry, symbolic notation, tabular data, annotations, and domain-specific text, forming a uniquely complex visual-textual domain core to engineering workflows. DrawingVQA bridges this gap with 33 "Issued for Construction" drawings and 92 expertly curated question-answer pairs, spanning three reasoning depths: perceptual understanding, contextual interpretation, and domain-expert reasoning. To evaluate model capabilities, we present a dual categorization framework to jointly analyze performance across seven construction-engineering and four MLLM capability dimensions -- the first to explicitly map engineering workflows to AI reasoning competencies. Evaluations of state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between model and expert performance, particularly at higher reasoning depths. This benchmark lays a foundation for domain-specialized multimodal reasoning to allow for advancement on integration of AI-driven understanding and real-world engineering workflows.

</details>


### [125] [Do Coding Agents Need Executable World Models, Simplification, and Verification to Solve ARC-AGI-3?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15439)
*Sergey Rodionov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过四个嵌套的Codex代理消融实验，系统分析了ARC-AGI-3智能体中可执行世界建模、调度简化和精确回放验证三大组件的贡献。完整验证方案在所有设置中排名第一，但资源消耗显著更高；在gpt-5.6-sol上该方案完全解决了所有公开游戏，约99% RHAE，动作数不到人类基线的一半。但模型数据后于游戏发布，该结果仅反映公开集饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清此前ARC-AGI-3智能体中三大组件（可执行世界建模、调度简化、精确回放验证）各自对整体性能的贡献，解决归因不明确的问题。

Method: 设计四个嵌套的Codex代理：文本基线、无回放验证的可执行世界模型、带调度简化的可执行模型、保留简化并要求精确复现观测的固定接口验证变体。在gpt-5.4/gpt-5.5上以高/极高推理努力评估，后续用gpt-5.6-sol探索文本和验证变体。

Result: 每个变体随模型增强和推理努力提升而改进；变体间差异小于预期；文本变体在gpt-5.5上优于灵活接口可执行变体；简化在四个设置中三个有效；完整验证在所有设置中排名第一但资源消耗大；gpt-5.6-sol上验证变体完全解决所有公开游戏，约99% RHAE，动作数不到人类基线一半。

Conclusion: 可执行交付物并非普遍有益；完整验证方案效果最强但成本最高；gpt-5.6-sol的结果因模型后于游戏发布且未见消融测试，仅反映公开集饱和，不能外推至泛化能力。

Abstract: Our previous ARC-AGI-3 agent bundled executable world modeling, scheduled simplification, and exact replay verification, leaving unclear which idea accounted for its performance. We address this attribution question with four nested Codex-based agents: a textual baseline; a flexible-interface executable world model without replay verification; the same executable model with scheduled simplification; and a fixed-interface verification treatment that retains simplification and requires exact reproduction of recorded observations. The main study evaluates all four agents with gpt-5.4 and gpt-5.5 at high and xhigh reasoning effort on the public ARC-AGI-3 games. Exploratory follow-ups evaluate the textual and verification variants with gpt-5.6-sol at xhigh and max. The most robust result is that every agent variant improves with a stronger model and with greater reasoning effort. Within each model-effort setting, differences among variants are smaller than anticipated, while the effects of individual components vary across settings. Requiring a persistent executable deliverable is not universally beneficial: the textual variant outperforms the flexible-interface executable variant in both gpt-5.5 settings. Simplification improves performance in three of the four model-effort settings, with the weakest setting as the only exception. The complete verification treatment ranks first in all four settings, although it uses substantially more resources. In the gpt-5.6-sol follow-up, the verification variant fully solves every public game at both reasoning efforts, achieves about 99% RHAE, and uses fewer than half the total actions of the human baseline. Because the model postdates these games and held-out performance remains untested, this result should be interpreted as saturation of the public set only.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond a Joke: Multi-Angle Reasoning for Detecting and Explaining Harmful Humor in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15442)
*Shanhong Liu,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh,Malika Meghjani,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAR-12框架，利用视觉语言模型从12个结构化视角分析网络迷因，结合角色感知软门控注意力机制和原型分类器，实现幽默与仇恨内容的检测及可解释性推理，在PrideMM和Memotion数据集上分别达到80.3%和75.9%的准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络迷因融合了视觉线索、文本内容和文化语境，尤其在幽默、讽刺与有害意图并存时难以解读。现有多模态分类器要么忽略这些相互依赖关系，要么仅提供有限的解释性，因此需要具备结构化推理能力的可解释迷因理解系统。

Method: MAR-12框架首先基于幽默与仇恨理论，从12个结构化视角对每个迷因进行解释；然后应用角色感知软门控注意力机制学习每个视角的贡献权重；接着通过原型分类器进行最终预测；最后结合视角特定推理和注意力权重合成解释，确保透明且基于上下文的合理性说明。

Result: 在PrideMM和Memotion数据集上，幽默检测准确率达80.3%，仇恨检测准确率达75.9%，均优于现有最先进方法。人类评估和GPT-4评估均证实MAR-12能生成连贯且具有说服力的解释，尤其适用于幽默与有害线索共存的情况。

Conclusion: MAR-12通过结构化视角建模和可解释注意力机制，有效解决了幽默与仇恨内容交织时的检测与理解难题，为迷因分析提供了兼具高性能和可解释性的新范式。

Abstract: Internet memes intertwine visual cues, textual content, and cultural context, making them particularly challenging to interpret in scenarios where humor, sarcasm, and harmful intent coexist. These complexities highlight the need for explainable meme understanding systems that can provide reliable and structured reasoning to support both accurate classification and human interpretability. However, existing multimodal classifiers either overlook these interdependencies or provide only limited interpretability. In this paper, we introduce MAR-12, a novel framework that leverages Vision Language Models (VLMs) for meme detection and understanding in settings where humorous and hateful elements may coexist. The framework first interprets each meme through twelve structured perspectives derived from humor and hate theories. It then applies a role-aware soft-gated attention mechanism to learn how much each perspective should contribute, followed by a prototype-based classifier for the final prediction. Finally, explanations are synthesized using both perspective-specific reasoning and learned attention weights, ensuring transparent and context-grounded justifications. We evaluate MAR-12 on the PrideMM and Memotion datasets, where it achieves up to 80.3% accuracy for humor detection and 75.9% accuracy for hate detection, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. Furthermore, both human and GPT-4-based evaluations confirm that MAR-12 produces coherent and persuasive explanations, particularly for memes in which humorous and harmful cues co-occur.

</details>


### [127] [From Black Box to Executable Logic: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Prolog Expert Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15459)
*Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将深度强化学习策略（PPO）转化为可解释的Prolog逻辑程序的三阶段后处理方法，并提供了理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略是黑盒模型，缺乏可解释性。作者希望将其转化为人类可读、逻辑引擎可执行、优化器可编辑的逻辑程序，从而实现可解释性。

Method: 三阶段后处理转化：(1) 提取冻结的PPO教师；(2) 通过经典关系学习从决策中归纳有序规则列表；(3) 将结果生成为Prolog程序。随后进行扩展阶段，通过策略评估编辑规则库，仅接受能提升回报的编辑。

Result: 理论上的四个保证：回报损失界、单调改进与终止、连续观测下阈值实例化的收敛率O(1/B)及匹配的指数级下界。实验上：在16,944个可达状态的双室钥匙门任务中达到精确最优回报；在Acrobot上用11条子句匹配神经教师；在CartPole恢复约97%回报；在LunarLander上部分恢复，符合指数下界预测。

Conclusion: 证明了深度强化学习策略可以被转化为可解释的逻辑程序，并具有理论保证，但连续控制任务中受观测维度的指数级成本限制。

Abstract: A trained deep reinforcement learning policy is a black box, and we ask whether it can be made explainable by rewriting it as an executable logic program that reproduces its behaviour and that a person can read, a logic engine can run, and an optimizer can edit. We present a three-stage post-hoc transformation that extracts a frozen proximal policy optimization teacher, induces an ordered rule list from its decisions in the manner of classical relational learning, and emits the result as a Prolog program whose every decision is executed by an off-the-shelf logic engine; a subsequent expansion stage edits the rule base and accepts an edit only when policy evaluation certifies a return increase. We prove four guarantees. A return-loss bound makes the distilled program a machine-checkable certificate in a finite Markov decision process, and the expansion loop improves monotonically and terminates. For the continuous-observation setting we answer whether the conversion is possible at all: the propositional threshold instantiation converts the network to arbitrary fidelity as the resolution B grows, with disagreement O(1/B) and a return gap that closes at the same rate, and a matching lower bound shows the cost is exponential in the observation dimension for an oblique decision boundary. Empirically, on a two-room key-and-door task with 16,944 reachable states the expanded Prolog program attains exact optimal return in every seed and, in a budget-capped regime, exceeds the stochastic teacher on exact return in ten of ten seeds. On three continuous-control tasks the emitted program substitutes the network, matching the neural teacher within noise on Acrobot with eleven clauses and recovering about 97% of its return on CartPole, while on the finer-control LunarLander it recovers only partially, exactly the ceiling the exponential lower bound predicts.

</details>


### [128] [A Critical Analysis of Trustworthy AI Tools, Mark Frameworks, and the Implementation Chasms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15480)
*Michael Papademas,Xenia Ziouvelou,Kostas Karpouzis,Vangelis Karkaletsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于OECD数据集，对可信人工智能（TAI）的操作化工具和信任标记框架进行了实证分析，发现当前工具在伦理关注、生命周期覆盖、利益相关方定位和工具类型上存在显著不对称：过度聚焦公平性、透明性和鲁棒性，忽视可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性；多数工具集中于开发后阶段，缺乏早期设计指导；教育和政策参与不足，呈现以行业技术措施为主导的格局。研究主张通过扩展伦理目标、嵌入AI全生命周期、促进多利益相关方参与来弥合原则与实践之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理指南虽然数量众多，但被批评过于抽象、缺乏具体的实施机制，存在原则与实践之间的鸿沟。本研究旨在系统诊断当前TAI操作化工具的分布现状与不足，为弥合这一鸿沟提供实证基础。

Method: 基于OECD数据集，采用经验性映射（empirical mapping）和描述性比较分析（descriptive comparative analysis）方法，对工具和信任标记框架进行系统分析。

Result: 发现伦理关注、生命周期覆盖、利益相关方定位和工具类型四方面存在显著不对称。公平性、透明性和鲁棒性受到高度关注，可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性被忽视；工具主要集中在开发后阶段，早期设计和数据收集阶段缺乏指导；教育和政策参与明显不足。

Conclusion: 弥合AI原则与实践之间的鸿沟需要：1）扩展伦理目标范围；2）将伦理嵌入AI全生命周期；3）促进更广泛的多利益相关方参与。研究提供了现有实施缺口的诊断和推进更全面、包容、可执行的AI治理的可行建议。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems increasingly impact society, ensuring their ethical and trustworthy deployment has become a global priority. While a myriad of high-level ethical guidelines have emerged, criticism persists that these frameworks remain abstract and lack concrete mechanisms for implementation. This paper conducts a critical analysis of tools and trust mark frameworks intended to operationalize trustworthy AI (TAI), drawing on a comprehensive dataset from the OECD. Through empirical mapping and descriptive comparative analysis, we identify significant asymmetries in ethical focus, lifecycle coverage, stakeholder targeting, and tool typology. Our findings show a strong emphasis on fairness, transparency, and robustness, with comparatively little attention paid to explainability, digital security, and environmental sustainability. Moreover, most tools and certifications concentrate on post-development stages, with limited guidance for early design or data collection phases. Educational initiatives and policy engagement are notably underdeveloped, suggesting that current TAI efforts are dominated by technical and procedural measures within industry contexts. We argue that bridging the persistent chasm between AI principles and practice requires expanding ethical objectives, embedding ethics across the AI lifecycle, and fostering broader multi-stakeholder participation. This study provides both a diagnosis of existing implementation gaps and actionable recommendations for advancing more holistic, inclusive, and enforceable AI governance

</details>


### [129] [Logic, Optimization, and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15532)
*J. N. Hooker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了逻辑与优化相结合在基于规则的AI中的应用，涵盖概率逻辑、贝叶斯逻辑、信念逻辑、非单调逻辑、多值逻辑及布尔回归等多种方法，并探讨了如何利用决策图和Benders分解计算投影、通过后最优性分析增强透明性，以及在回答集编程中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI透明性（可复现性、可解释性、可信赖性、公平性）日益受到重视，基于规则的AI因其天然透明性而重新获得关注。逻辑是编码规则库和进行推理的媒介，而优化提供了强大的推理计算技术，二者的结合有望在提升AI透明性的同时保持高效的推理能力。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了逻辑-优化伙伴关系在多个领域的应用：概率逻辑、贝叶斯逻辑、信念逻辑与Dempster-Shafer理论、非单调（默认）逻辑、多值逻辑、从含噪数据中推断逻辑公式的布尔回归。展示了如何使用决策图和基于逻辑的Benders分解计算投影，以及后最优性分析和回答集编程模理论中的优化方法。

Result: 文章揭示了逻辑与优化结合在多个逻辑推理范式中的有效性，特别是在增强透明性方面。决策图和Benders分解可用于高效计算投影问题（逻辑与优化的基本问题），后最优性分析有助于解释推理结论，优化在回答集编程模理论中也扮演重要角色。

Conclusion: 逻辑与优化的结合为基于规则的透明AI提供了可行且日益实用的技术路径，随着优化方法的进步，这一方向具有广阔前景，文章最后指出了若干未来研究方向。

Abstract: Logic and optimization can, in combination, make valuable contributions to rule-based AI. Logic is the obvious medium for encoding a rule base and drawing inferences from it, while optimization provides a powerful technology for computing inferences. Their combination has taken on new relevance amid a growing concern for transparency in AI. which is important for reproducibility, explainability, trustworthiness, and fairness. Rule-based AI provides a natural solution to transparency that is becoming increasingly practical due to today's highly advanced optimization methods. This article surveys several areas of logic-optimization partnership, including probabilistic logic, Bayesian logic, belief logics and Dempster-Shafer theory, nonmonotonic (default) logic, many-valued logics, and inference of logical formulas from noisy data based on Boolean regression. It shows how to compute projections, the fundamental problem of both logic and optimization, using decision diagrams and logic-based Benders decomposition. It describes the use of postoptimality analysis to explain how conclusions are reached, further enhancing transparency, as well as the role of optimization in answer set programming modulo theories. The paper concludes by suggesting possible future research directions.

</details>


### [130] [SeerGuard: A Safety Framework for Mobile GUI Agents via World Model Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15550)
*Xue Yu,Bo Yuan,Pengshuai Yang,Kailin Zhao,Hong Hu,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeerGuard是一个后果感知的安全框架，通过执行前的指令级筛选和动作级风险评估来降低移动GUI代理的安全风险。它构建了统一的安全增强世界模型（SAWM），集成语义下一状态预测与安全风险评估。实验表明，SeerGuard能有效提升移动GUI代理的安全-效用评分（从0.191提升至0.596），并将风险成本评分从0.347降至0.130。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动GUI代理的安全机制主要是反应式的，缺乏在执行前评估风险的能力，而一个错误动作可能导致不可逆的严重后果。

Method: 提出SeerGuard框架，包括两个核心机制：1）执行前的指令级筛选；2）动作级风险评估。通过多任务学习构建统一的安全增强世界模型（SAWM），整合语义下一状态预测与安全风险评估。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，将安全-效用评分从0.191提升至0.596（ω=0.8），风险成本评分从0.347降至0.130（α=0.8）。在多种移动GUI代理上均展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SeerGuard作为一种后果感知的预防性安全框架，能有效降低移动GUI代理的安全风险，且具有良好的跨代理泛化能力。

Abstract: Mobile graphical user interface (GUI) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in automating complex tasks, yet they introduce critical safety risks where a single erroneous action can lead to irreversible consequences. Existing safety mechanisms are primarily reactive, lacking the ability to assess risks before execution. In this paper, we introduce SeerGuard, a consequence-aware safety framework designed to mitigate these risks through pre-execution instruction-level screening and action-level risk assessment. Specifically, the action-level assessment analyzes agent-proposed actions within current GUI states, anticipating likely outcomes to identify risks before they are executed. To enable these capabilities, we construct a unified safety-augmented world model (SAWM) via multi-task learning, integrating semantic next-state prediction with safety risk assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SeerGuard generalizes effectively across diverse mobile GUI agents. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, it increases the safety-utility score from $0.191$ to $0.596$ at $ω=0.8$ and reduces the risk-cost score from $0.347$ to $0.130$ at $α=0.8$. Further analyses on our SAWM validate the effectiveness of the instruction-level screening, alongside the capability of action risk assessment and next-state prediction.

</details>


### [131] [MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15592)
*Xu Hou,Meiyu Liang,Wei Huang,Yawen Li,Zhe Xue,Wu Liu,Guanhua Ye,Lei Shi,Kangkang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MGDT框架，采用“先对齐再扩散”的范式解决多模态知识图谱补全问题。通过关系自适应语义路由专家混合模块选择关系相关模态信息，利用多模态大语言模型对齐跨模态语义，最后用知识图谱扩散Transformer在对齐空间生成缺失实体。在三个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的MKGC方法直接在原始多模态特征上降噪，迫使降噪器同时执行关系相关线索选择、跨模态语义对齐和结构感知实体生成，导致条件噪声和语义不一致，性能次优。

Method: 提出MGDT框架，包含：(1) RASR-MoE模块选择关系相关的多模态语义变换路径并抑制无关模态干扰；(2) 冻结的MLLM作为语义锚点，将路由后的多模态表示对齐到统一潜在空间，减少跨模态语义异质性；(3) KGDT在对齐空间执行图条件降噪生成，输出缺失实体表示。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，MGDT一致性地优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过“先对齐再扩散”的范式，将关系相关模态选择、跨模态语义对齐与结构感知生成解耦，有效提升了多模态知识图谱补全性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion (MKGC) requires inferring missing entities from structural, textual, and visual cues. Existing diffusion-based MKGC methods usually denoise directly on raw multimodal features. Such a design forces the denoiser to simultaneously perform relation-dependent cue selection, cross-modal semantic alignment, and structure-aware entity generation, which introduces noisy and semantically inconsistent conditions for diffusion and consequently leads to suboptimal completion performance. To address this limitation, we propose MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts (MGDT), a novel MKGC framework built on an align-then-diffuse paradigm. MGDT first employs a Relation-Adaptive Semantic Routing Mixture-of-Experts (RASR-MoE) module to select relation-relevant multimodal semantic transformation paths and suppress irrelevant modality interference. MGDT then uses a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a semantic anchor to align the routed multimodal representations into a unified latent space and reduce cross-modal semantic heterogeneity. Finally, a Knowledge Graph Diffusion Transformer (KGDT) performs graph-conditioned denoising generation in the aligned space to produce the missing entity representation. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that MGDT consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Neuro-Symbolic AI for LEED compliance: Document-Centric Benchmarking, Deterministic Numeric Checking, and When Multimodal Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15647)
*Aritro De,Juliana Felkner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号流水线，使用本地部署的4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）结合确定性数值检查器，对LEED v4.1 BD+C认证文档进行合规性筛查。在4栋大学建筑（484份PDF，153项信用决策）的实验中，文本核心验证器达到67.3%准确率，完整流水线61.6%准确率。低分辨率图纸会降低性能，提示策略的效果取决于项目文档丰富度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEED v4.1 BD+C认证过程文档密集、依赖人工审阅，需要逐页阅读证据并手动应用信用阈值逻辑，缺乏自动化筛选工具。研究旨在探索小规模本地部署语言模型能否进行有意义的LEED文档筛查，以及确定性符号组件应如何分担工作。

Method: 提出神经符号流水线：1) 将项目PDF对齐到LEED信用章节；2) 使用信用感知关键词签名检索证据；3) 通过本地4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）验证合规性；4) 应用LEED特定数值检查器处理定量阈值。在4栋大学建筑上进行系统消融实验，对比不同模型规模、提示策略、图像分辨率的影响。

Result: 4B参数模型（gemma3:4b）以67.3%准确率超越8B参数模型（llama3.1:8b）。确定性数值检查器将EA-p2准确率从50%提升至100%。完整神经符号流水线准确率61.6%，落后于文本基线。低分辨率图纸（150-300 dpi）持续降低准确率。提示效果取决于项目真实通过率：rubric提示在文档丰富项目上最优，chain-of-thought在文档贫乏项目上最优。

Conclusion: 小规模本地语言模型可在有限范围内有效辅助LEED文档筛查，但存在提取失败和定性类别保守行为的局限。该流水线和基线为LEED v4.1 BD+C合规性验证提供了可重复的参考基准，明确了准确率与失败模式。

Abstract: LEED v4.1 BD+C certification remains a document-intensive process that requires reviewers to read hundreds of pages of project evidence and apply credit-specific threshold logic by hand. This paper investigates whether small, locally deployed language models can perform meaningful screening of LEED documentation and how deterministic symbolic components should share that work. A neuro-symbolic pipeline is introduced that aligns project PDFs to LEED credit sections, retrieves evidence with credit-aware keyword signatures, verifies compliance with a locally hosted 4-billion-parameter language model, and applies a LEED-specific numeric checker to quantitative thresholds. Experiments on four university buildings (484 PDFs, 153 credit-level decisions) show that a 4-billion-parameter model (gemma3:4b) is the strongest text-only core verifier, achieving 67.3% accuracy and outperforming a larger 8-billion-parameter model (llama3.1:8b) in this task. The deterministic numeric checker corrects arithmetic errors on key quantitative credits, moving EA-p2 from 50% to 100% accuracy and improving several other credits when required values are reliably extracted. At the same time, the full neuro-symbolic configuration achieves 61.6% overall accuracy, trailing the best text-only baseline due to extraction failures and conservative behavior on qualitative categories. Systematic ablations show that adding low-resolution drawing images (150-300 dpi) consistently reduces accuracy, and that prompt effectiveness depends on the building's ground-truth PASS rate: rubric prompts perform best on documentation-rich projects, while chain-of-thought prompts perform best on documentation-lean projects. Within the specific scope of LEED v4.1 BD+C compliance verification over raw project documentation, this pipeline and its baselines provide an initial reproducible reference point for both accuracy and failure modes.

</details>


### [133] [ToolVerse: Unlocking Massive Environments and Long-Horizon Tasks for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15660)
*Shuaiyu Zhou,Fengpeng Yue,Zengjie Hu,Yuanzhe Shen,Chenyang Zhang,feng hong,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolVerse是一个通过强化学习训练LLM Agent在复杂工具集成环境中进行长程推理的框架，包含400个MCP协议、约4500个工具，并提出GUST数据集和Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法来解决信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent在紧凑、定义良好的场景中表现良好，但在需要无缝工具集成的大规模、多样化的动态真实环境中鲁棒性和有效性不足。

Method: (1) 从近400个真实MCP协议自动构建包含约4500个工具的大规模可执行Agent训练环境；(2) 基于工具依赖图的任务设计策略，使用Dynamic Unlocking Sampling算法生成长程任务，构建GUST数据集；(3) 提出细粒度的Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法缓解长程Agent RL中的信用分配问题。

Result: 在多个Agent基准上进行RL训练，实验结果表明框架显著增强了LLM在长程工具使用上的能力，实现了性能提升并在动态环境中展现出鲁棒推理能力。

Conclusion: ToolVerse有效弥合了LLM Agent在简单场景与复杂动态真实环境之间的差距，为工具集成推理任务中的长程强化学习训练提供了可行的规模化解决方案。

Abstract: While LLM agents demonstrate strong reasoning abilities in compact and well-defined scenarios, they struggle to maintain robustness and effectiveness when faced with large-scale, diverse, and dynamic real-world environments that demand seamless tool integration. To address this gap, we introduce ToolVerse, a comprehensive framework that scales up agentic RL environments and enables agents to perform complex long-horizon reasoning in Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) tasks. First, ToolVerse automatically builds the massive executable agent training environments from nearly 400 real-world Model Context Protocols (MCPs) that contain about 4500 tools. Second, we propose a task design strategy based on a tool dependency graph, utilizing Dynamic Unlocking Sampling Algorithm to generate long-horizon tasks, and produce GUST (Graph Unlocking Sampling Tasks) dataset. Third, to alleviate the credit assigment problem in long-horizon agentic RL, we propose a fine-grained Turn-Aware Relative Advantage algorithm. We conduct extensive Agentic RL training using ToolVerse and evaluate our framework on serveral agentic benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly strengthens LLMs' capabilities in long-horizon tool use, achieving a marked performance boost and showcasing robust reasoning within dynamic environments.

</details>


### [134] [S1-Omni: A Unified Multimodal Reasoning Model for Scientific Understanding, Prediction, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15686)
*Jiahao Zhao,Junyi Liu,Lifeng Xu,Nan Xu,Qingli Wang,Qingxiao Li,Tianle Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Yawen Zheng,Zikai Wang,Guanming Liu,Hequn Zhou,Jingyi Wang,Jingyuan Shu,Keqi Wang,Li He,Songyang Diao,Wenhui Xu,Xinyu Ren,Yaqin Fan,Yujin Zhou,Zhanao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S1-Omni是一个统一的科学多模态推理模型，通过共享表示空间整合分子结构、序列、光谱和图像等多种科学数据，在200个科学任务上训练，在60多个基准测试上超越GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，部分任务达到或超过领域专用模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI for Science领域存在严重的碎片化问题，不同模型各自处理特定类型的科学数据（如分子结构、蛋白质序列、光谱等），缺乏统一建模能力，无法对异构数据、科学定律和专家知识进行联合建模。

Method: S1-Omni包含三大核心组件：1）统一表示空间：将自然语言指令与CIF、SMILES、蛋白质序列、光谱、科学图像等映射到共享表示空间；2）自然世界知识对齐：将科学定律和专家知识融入数据构建和训练过程；3）任务特定解码：支持属性预测、光谱到分子生成、蛋白质位点和结构预测、科学图像生成与编辑等多种任务。

Result: 在S1-Omni-Corpus（涵盖200个科学任务、数百万推理样本）上训练，在60多个科学基准测试上评估，在大多数基准上优于GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，在多个基准上与领域专用模型持平或更优。

Conclusion: S1-Omni为统一的科学建模提供了可行路径，证明了将异构科学数据、科学定律和专家知识整合到单一推理模型中的有效性。

Abstract: We present S1-Omni, a unified multimodal reasoning model for scientific understanding, prediction, and generation. AI for Science (AI4S) has advanced significantly through domain-specific models, tool-augmented LLMs, and scientific language models. However, model capabilities remain highly fragmented, limiting the joint modeling of heterogeneous data, scientific laws, and expert knowledge. S1-Omni addresses this gap by consolidating these capabilities into a single, coherent scientific reasoning model. The architecture of S1-Omni is built upon three core components: unified representation of scientific data, natural-world knowledge alignment, and decoding for domain-specific tasks. First, S1-Omni maps natural-language instructions and scientific objects, including CIF, SMILES, protein sequences, spectra, and scientific images, into a shared representation space. Second, it incorporates scientific laws and expert knowledge into data construction and training, enabling the model to reason from scientific evidence. Third, it performs task-specific decoding to support a broad range of applications, including property prediction, spectrum-to-molecular generation, protein site and structure prediction, and scientific image generation and editing. S1-Omni is trained on S1-Omni-Corpus, which covers 200 scientific tasks and contains millions of reasoning samples, and is evaluated on over 60 scientific benchmarks. It outperforms GPT-5.5 and Gemini-3.1-Pro on most benchmarks and matches or surpasses domain-specific models on several benchmarks. Overall, S1-Omni provides a practical path toward unified scientific modeling.

</details>


### [135] [Behavioral Controllability of Agentic Models for Information Extraction: From Fixed Workflows to Reflective Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15715)
*Lujia Zhang,Xingzhou Chen,Hongwei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体组件（反思、记忆等）在信息抽取任务中是否带来可观察、可控制的改进，通过会议论文数据集抽取任务，比较固定工作流与反思型智能体变体，并设计了优化智能体条件（S2），重点分析过程级行为（工具执行、重试、反思、内存使用、运行时间和故障恢复），同时将抽取覆盖率和字段完整性作为次要结果指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体被广泛用于复杂信息抽取任务，但反思、记忆等智能体组件是否能在固定LLM工作流基础上带来可观察且可控的改进尚不明确，本文旨在系统性地回答这一问题。

Method: 通过会议论文数据集抽取任务，比较固定工作流基线、反思型智能体变体以及优化智能体条件（S2，引入更丰富的PDF工具和动态工具选择），重点评估过程级行为指标（工具执行、重试、反思、内存使用、运行时间、故障恢复），同时以抽取覆盖率和字段完整性作为次要指标。

Result: 论文刻画了智能体机制何时改变系统行为、这些改变是否改进任务完成度，以及观察到的失败模式如何驱动在相同评估框架下的优化智能体设计。

Conclusion: 智能体组件会改变系统行为，但这种改变并不一定带来任务完成度的提升；失败模式的系统化分析有助于指导更优智能体架构的设计。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for complex information-extraction tasks, yet it remains unclear whether agentic components such as reflection and memory lead to observable and controllable improvements over fixed LLM workflows. We study this question through conference-paper dataset extraction, where a system must identify datasets mentioned in scholarly PDFs and produce structured records. We compare a fixed workflow baseline with reflective agent variants and specify an optimized agent condition (S2) that extends the same task with richer PDF tools and dynamic tool selection. Our evaluation emphasizes process-level behavior--including tool execution, retries, reflection, memory use, runtime, and failure recovery--while treating extraction coverage and field completeness as secondary outcome measures. The paper characterizes when agentic mechanisms change system behavior, whether these changes improve task completion, and how the observed failure modes motivate an optimized agent design under the same evaluation harness.

</details>


### [136] [NeurOWL: An LLM-Based Neural-symbolic Framework for Incomplete OWL Ontology Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15776)
*Hui Yang,Jiaoyan Chen,Yiping Song,Renate Schmidt,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeurOWL，一个端到端的神经符号框架，用于解决不完整OWL本体中的蕴含关系推理问题，联合进行子类关系验证和溯因推理，利用大语言模型和本体嵌入同时捕捉形式语义和文本语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的OWL本体往往不完整，导致标准推理器无法进行有效的子类关系推理；现有方法缺乏统一的框架同时解决验证和溯因问题。

Method: 提出NeurOWL，一个端到端的神经符号框架，结合LLM和本体嵌入，联合执行子类关系验证（判断语义合理性）和溯因推理（提供包含潜在缺失公理的解释），无需预定义候选缺失公理集。

Result: 在多个领域的真实本体上进行了评估，展示了强健且跨领域稳定的性能。

Conclusion: NeurOWL有效地统一了子类关系验证和本体溯因推理，在不完整本体上实现了语义合理的推理和可解释的结果。

Abstract: OWL ontologies provide a formal knowledge representation framework that enables semantic reasoning, and have been widely adopted across domains such as healthcare and bioinformatics. In practice, however, real-world ontologies are often incomplete, which pose challenges for reasoning. In this work, we focus on a fundamental subsumption reasoning problem: given an incomplete ontology and a candidate (non-entailed) subsumption, determine whether the subsumption is semantically plausible and, if so, providing a logically sound explanation containing potential missing axioms. This task unifies subsumption verification with ontology abduction, and generalizes the latter by removing the need for a predefined candidate set of missing axioms. To address this subsumption reasoning problem, we propose NeurOWL, an end-to-end neuro-symbolic framework that jointly performs verification and abduction, leveraging both formally defined semantics and textual semantics through Large Language Models and ontology embeddings. We evaluate NeurOWL on real-world ontologies across multiple domains, demonstrating strong and robust performance across different domains.

</details>


### [137] [AgentFAIR: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for FAIRness Evaluation of Geospatial Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15781)
*Ming Chen,Pranav Pai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentFAIR多智能体框架，结合结构化元数据提取和13个LLM评估器进行FAIR原则评估，在50个数据集上平均得分偏差15个百分点，专家一致性达82%，每次评估成本约0.054美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FAIR合规评估工具在评估标准、证据来源上存在差异，且无法处理JavaScript渲染页面或特定仓库标识符，导致评估结果不一致（标准化得分标准差平均15个百分点，最高30.3），需要更一致、可审计的评估方法。

Method: 提出AgentFAIR多智能体框架：结合结构化元数据提取与13个针对FAIR子原则的LLM评估器，每个评估器输出0-3成熟度评分、引用证据和改进建议；另设批评智能体检查证据一致性和推理，必要时请求重新评估。

Result: 50个数据集的平均可查找性79.7%、可访问性70.4%、可互操作性45.3%、可重用性72.0%；与四种基线工具秩相关系数0.31-0.61；子原则一致性89%（标准差3%），优于无批评智能体的71%；专家研究Fleiss' kappa=0.71，一致性82%；API成本约0.054美元/数据集。

Conclusion: AgentFAIR支持审计性和可行性，但由于基准有限、消融不完整、仅验证单模型族，准确性及泛化性的结论受到限制。

Abstract: Geospatial datasets support applications from urban planning to climate modeling, yet consistent assessment of FAIR compliance is difficult. Existing evaluators use different rubrics and evidence sources and may fail on JavaScript-rendered pages or repository-specific identifiers. For 50 datasets from 10 repositories, the standard deviation of normalized scores across available tools averages 15.0 percentage points and reaches 30.3 for one dataset. Because these outputs are not equivalent measurements, we use them to characterize disagreement and failure modes, not comparative accuracy. We present AgentFAIR, a multi-agent framework combining structured metadata extraction with 13 sub-principle-specific LLM evaluators. Each produces a 0-3 maturity score, cited evidence, and recommendations; a critic checks evidence and consistency and can request targeted re-evaluation. Mean Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability scores are 79.7%, 70.4%, 45.3%, and 72.0%. Rank correlations with four baseline tools range from 0.31 to 0.61; the FAIR-enough comparison is not statistically significant. On a 10-dataset repeated-run subset, sub-principle agreement averages 89% (standard deviation: 3 percentage points), versus 71% without the critic. A preliminary 15-dataset expert study yields Fleiss' kappa of 0.71 and 82% alignment with expert consensus. API cost is approximately USD 0.054 per dataset. These results support auditability and feasibility, while the limited benchmark, incomplete ablations, and single-model-family validation constrain claims about accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [138] [Knowledge-Centric Agents for Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15845)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Ruibo Ming,He Zhang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识驱动的工作流生成框架，通过知识反转、注入和推理三个层次，将大型语言模型从直接的文本到JSON生成转变为层次化知识建模，在ComfyUI等视觉创作系统中实现了更丰富、更连贯、执行成功率更高的工作流生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法将工作流生成视为直接的文本到JSON任务，存在结构脆弱性问题，且缺乏专家级别的模块组合推理经验和层次化设计知识。

Method: 提出知识驱动框架，包含三步：1) 知识反转——从真实工作流中提取层次化表示（伪代码、骨架、高层策略）；2) 知识注入——通过监督微调让模型学习从任务描述到策略、再从策略到可执行结构的推理链条；3) 推理阶段——可逆推理合成可执行工作流，并辅以自精炼保证结构一致性。

Result: 该方法相比现有系统，生成的工作流具有更丰富的节点多样性、更连贯的结构以及更高的执行成功率。

Conclusion: 该工作为知识驱动的智能体工作流生成奠定了新基础，证明了层次化知识建模相对于直接生成范式的优势。

Abstract: Workflow generation in visual creation systems such as ComfyUI demands not only syntactic accuracy but also expert-level reasoning over modular compositions. Existing large language model (LLM) approaches often treat this as a direct text-to-JSON generation task, struggling with structural brittleness and lacking the experiential knowledge required for effective design. We argue that successful workflow generation requires modeling knowledge itself, including its structure, hierarchy, and reasoning dynamics. To this end, we propose a knowledge-centric framework that learns to invert, inject, and infer with knowledge across multiple abstraction levels. We first perform knowledge inversion to distill hierarchical representations, ranging from full pseudo-codes and skeletons to high-level strategies, from large collections of real-world workflows. We then conduct knowledge injection through supervised fine-tuning, teaching the model to reason from task descriptions to strategies and from strategies to executable structures. During inference, the model performs reversible reasoning to synthesize executable workflows, augmented by self-refinement for structural coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces workflows with richer node diversity, more coherent structures, and higher execution success rates than existing systems, establishing a new foundation for knowledge-driven, agentic workflow generation.

</details>


### [139] [DSWorld: A Data Science World Model for Efficient Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15901)
*Zherui Yang,Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据科学世界模型(Data Science World Model)概念及其实践框架DSWorld，通过预测数据科学操作的环境状态转移来加速数据科学智能体的工作流程，无需反复执行实际计算。结合结构化状态构建、成本感知路由、轻量级执行和LLM模拟器，并引入反思式世界模型优化(Reflective World Model Optimization)进行训练。实验表明，DSWorld可将强化学习训练加速约14倍，搜索推理加速3-6倍，并在状态转移预测任务上超越最强LLM基线35.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主数据科学智能体依赖反复试错的工作流程，涉及大量昂贵计算，效率低下。需要一种能够在不执行实际运算的情况下预测数据科学操作效果的模型，从而避免不必要的计算开销。

Method: 提出DSWorld框架，包括：(1) 结构化状态构建，将工作流状态编码为结构化表示；(2) 成本感知路由，区分轻量级与重量级操作；(3) 轻量级实时执行，对低成本操作直接执行；(4) LLM模拟器，对昂贵操作进行预测模拟。同时构建8K规模的状态转移轨迹数据集，并提出反思式世界模型优化(RWMO)，一种基于错误感知的强化学习策略来改进转移预测。

Result: DSWorld将基于强化学习的智能体训练加速约14倍，基于搜索的推理加速约3-6倍，同时保持有竞争力的任务性能。在状态转移预测任务上，超越最强LLM基线35.6%。

Conclusion: 提出数据科学世界模型这一新概念，并通过DSWorld框架验证其有效性，为解决数据科学智能体计算效率瓶颈提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite strong capabilities in data understanding and decision-making, autonomous data science agents still heavily rely on trial-and-error workflows that involve expensive computation. This bottleneck motivates models that can anticipate the effects of data science operations before real execution. In this paper, we introduce the concept of Data Science World Model, which model the data science execution environment by predicting environment state transitions conditioned on current workflow states and candidate operations. We further propose DSWorld, a practical framework that combines structured state construction, cost-aware routing, lightweight real execution, and an LLM-based simulator for expensive operations. To support training, we construct an 8K-scale transition trajectory dataset and introduce Reflective World Model Optimization, an error-aware reinforcement learning strategy for improving transition prediction. Experiments show that DSWorld accelerates RL-based agent training by approximately $14\times$ and search-based inference by approximately $3$-$6\times$ while maintaining competitive performance, and outperforms the strongest LLM baseline by 35.6% on transition prediction tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DSWorld.

</details>


### [140] [A Formally Grounded ODRL Evaluator: Implementation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15987)
*Jaime Osvaldo Salas,Paolo Pareti,Adeel Aslam,Christopher Maidens,George Konstantinidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对ODRL策略语言缺乏形式化语义导致互操作性差的问题，提出了首个支持所有规则类型、具有透明形式语义的ODRL评估器，并通过实验证明了其在静态和流式场景下的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODRL策略语言已成为欧洲数据空间中数据访问、AI治理策略等政策建模的事实标准，但当前标准缺乏数学形式语义来描述系统应如何实现策略评估，导致不同系统和工具对语言有不同的解释，限制了互操作性且无法保证结果一致性。

Method: 基于现有的ODRL语义模型，形式化了ODRL在访问控制和监控场景下的评估问题（包括静态和流式设置），提出了一种新颖高效的算法并实现，构建了首个支持所有规则类型且具有透明形式语义的ODRL评估器。

Result: 通过实验测量了性能，分析了与策略复杂度和评估数据规模相关的不同可扩展性维度，并与现有ODRL评估器进行了比较，展示了在支持的ODRL特性和评估模式上的差异。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ODRL评估器是首个具有透明形式语义且支持所有规则类型的实现，解决了现有系统缺乏形式语义导致的互操作性问题，在性能上具有优势，填补了该领域空白。

Abstract: The ODRL policy language is emerging as the de-facto standard for policy modelling data access and usage preferences, AI governance policies and data workflows in European dataspaces. The current standard has no mathematical formal semantics to describe how a system should implement policy evaluation. This has resulted in a variety of systems and tools that implement their own interpretation of the language, which limits interoperability and cannot guarantee consistent results. Based on an existing semantic model of ODRL, we formalise the problems of ODRL evaluation for the access control and monitoring scenarios, in both static and streaming settings, and we provide a novel, efficient algorithm and implementation. We present the first ODRL Evaluator with transparent formal semantics and supporting all rule types. We experimentally measure its performance, analysing different scalability dimensions related to policy complexity and size of the data on which a policy is evaluated. We compare our system with the state-of-the-art by providing a comparative review of existing ODRL evaluators, which highlights the differences in supported ODRL features and evaluation modes.

</details>


### [141] [Closing the AI Trust Gap: The Case for Independent Certification for Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15992)
*Trisevgeni Papakonstantinou,Cansu Canca,Farah Nanji,Waheedullah Pardess,Jen Weedon,Jasmijn Remmers,Eliza Krigman,Matthew Ball,Yalda Daryani,Kiran Iqbal,Francielle Vargas,María Llorente Sánchez,Joe Humphreys,Fendi Tsim,Kelly Fitzpatrick,Jeff Dunn,Catherine Feldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，尽管负责任AI（RAI）实践已积累大量风险识别与缓解方法，但未能建立起奖励可信赖性的市场机制。企业投入安全、公平和监管的努力无法向消费者、监管者和股东有效证明其超越最低合规标准，导致“信任差距”——一种结构性问题：内部负责任开发无法产生外部独立认可的可验证信号。作者认为，这一差距部分源于关注“负责任AI”（内部流程）而非“可信AI”（独立可验证的真实世界结果），并归结为三个失败：市场无法区分可信系统与仿制品；评估针对模型和输出而非部署的社会技术系统及其结果；测量体系以规避伤害而非展示收益为导向。通过对比医疗、可持续性和安全领域的认证制度，作者提出独立的、以结果为导向的认证作为弥合信任差距的连接层，使可信赖性变得可测量、可比较并具有商业回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前负责任AI实践存在“信任差距”——内部负责任开发无法产生外部可验证的可信信号，导致市场无法奖励真正的可信赖性，亟需建立一种外部、独立、结果导向的认证机制来弥合这一结构性缺陷。

Method: 本文采用比较分析方法，将现有AI治理工具与医疗、可持续性和安全领域的成熟认证制度进行系统性对比，识别当前AI治理框架中缺乏的关键要素：治理基线、独立验证的积极结果证据和市场信号。

Result: 现有AI治理工具均未能在单个框架中整合治理基线、独立验证的积极结果证据和市场信号三个要素；认证体系相对于审计、标签、自我声明等工具，更能提供可比较、可验证的可信度信号。

Conclusion: 独立的、以结果为导向的认证是弥合信任差距的关键连接层，能够补充现有的内部治理和监管体系，使可信赖性变得可测量、可比较并具有商业回报，从而激励企业超越最低合规标准。

Abstract: Over the past decade, responsible AI (RAI) has produced a substantial body of practice for identifying and mitigating the risks AI poses in high-stakes settings. Yet this work has not produced a market that rewards trustworthiness. Firms that invest seriously in safety, fairness, and oversight cannot consistently prove to consumers, regulators, and shareholders that their systems go beyond the bare minimum of compliance. What is missing is a way for society to recognize or compare the difference. The result is a trust gap: a structural condition in which responsible development efforts happen inside organizations but produce no external, independently recognized and verifiable signal of trustworthy outcomes. We argue this gap is sustained in part because of a focus on responsible AI (a matter of internal process) as opposed to trustworthy AI (a matter of independently verifiable real-world outcomes), and that it persists because of three compounding failures: (1) the market cannot distinguish trustworthy systems from their imitations; (2) evaluation targets models and outputs rather than deployed sociotechnical systems and their outcomes; (3) the measurement ecosystem is oriented toward avoiding harm rather than demonstrating benefit.
